  function earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx){

    }
    return lo<levels.length&&levels[lo]<=mx;
  }
  function earthlineMeasureContourCells15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr){

  function earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales){
    const mask=new Uint8Array(Math.max(1,N));
    const mark=(x,y)=>{
      const ix=Math.round(x),iy=Math.round(y);
      if(ix>=0&&iy>=0&&ix<GW&&iy<GH)mask[idx(ix,iy)]=1;
    };
    for(const sw of (swales||[])){
      const pts=sw&&sw.pts||[];
      for(let j=0;j<pts.length;j++){
        mark(pts[j][0],pts[j][1]);
        if(j===0)continue;
        const a=pts[j-1],b=pts[j];
        const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0]),Math.abs(b[1]-a[1]))*2));
        for(let k=1;k<steps;k++){
          const t=k/steps;mark(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t);
        }
      }
    }
    return mask;
  }
  function earthlineEDT1D15803(f,n){
    const v=new Int32Array(n),z=new Float64Array(n+1),d=new Float64Array(n);
    let k=0;v[0]=0;z[0]=-Infinity;z[1]=Infinity;
    for(let q=1;q<n;q++){
      let s;
      do{
        const p=v[k];
        s=((f[q]+q*q)-(f[p]+p*p))/(2*q-2*p);
        if(s<=z[k])k--;
      }while(s<=z[k]&&k>=0);
      k++;v[k]=q;z[k]=s;z[k+1]=Infinity;
    }
    k=0;
    for(let q=0;q<n;q++){
      while(z[k+1]<q)k++;
      const p=v[k];d[q]=(q-p)*(q-p)+f[p];
    }
    return d;
  }
  function earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask){
    const BIG=1e12,tmp=new Float64Array(N),out=new Float64Array(N);
    for(let y=0;y<GH;y++){

      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)f[x]=corridorMask[idx(x,y)]?0:BIG;
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)tmp[idx(x,y)]=d[x];
    }
    for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)f[y]=tmp[idx(x,y)];
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)out[idx(x,y)]=Math.sqrt(d[y])*Math.max(.001,Number(M.cellM||1));
    }
    return out;
  }

  function earthlineComputeCoverage15803(diag,swales,maxS,chanThr){
    const corridorMask=earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales);
    let corridorPixels=0;for(const v of corridorMask)corridorPixels+=v?1:0;
    const thresholds=[
      {key:"within25ft",ft:25,m:25/3.280839895},
      {key:"within50ft",ft:50,m:50/3.280839895},
      {key:"within100ft",ft:100,m:100/3.280839895}
    ];
    const counts={within25ft:0,within50ft:0,within100ft:0};
    const dist=corridorPixels?earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask):null;
    let safe=0;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS||!Number.isFinite(Number(M.h[i])))continue;
      safe++;
      const z=earthlineCoverageZone15803(diag,x,y);
      const dm=dist?dist[i]:Infinity;
      for(const t of thresholds)if(dm<=t.m){counts[t.key]++;z.coverage[t.key]++;}
    }
    const pct={};
    for(const t of thresholds)pct[t.key]=safe?100*counts[t.key]/safe:0;
    for(const z of diag.zones){
      for(const t of thresholds)z.coverage[t.key+"Pct"]=z.safeCells?100*z.coverage[t.key]/z.safeCells:0;
    }
    diag.coverage={
      method:"exact Euclidean grid distance to rasterized accepted corridor centerlines",
      cellM:Number(M.cellM||0),safeCells:safe,corridorPixels,
      counts,pct,
      within25ftPct:pct.within25ft,
      within50ftPct:pct.within50ft,
      within100ftPct:pct.within100ft
    };
  }

  function earthlineFinalizeCandidateAccounting15840(diag){

      zone15840.flowFirstCandidates=selection15840.filter(r=>r.zone===zone15840.id&&r.candidateOrigin15840==="flow-first").length;

  function earthlineFinalizeSeedFates15840(diag){

  function earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx){
    let lo=0,hi=levels.length-1;
    while(lo<=hi){
      const mid=(lo+hi)>>1,v=levels[mid];
      if(v<mn)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid-1;
    }
    return lo<levels.length&&levels[lo]<=mx;
  }
  function earthlineMeasureContourCells15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr){
    if(!diag||!levels||!levels.length)return;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS)continue;
      const h00=Number(M.h[idx(x,y)]),h10=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y)]);
      const h01=Number(M.h[idx(x,y+1)]),h11=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y+1)]);
      if(![h00,h10,h01,h11].every(Number.isFinite))continue;
      const mn=Math.min(h00,h10,h01,h11),mx=Math.max(h00,h10,h01,h11);
      if(mx-mn>1e-7&&earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx)){
        earthlineCoverageZone15803(diag,x,y).contourCells++;
        diag.totals.contourCells++;
      }
    }
  }
  function earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales){
    const mask=new Uint8Array(Math.max(1,N));
    const mark=(x,y)=>{
      const ix=Math.round(x),iy=Math.round(y);
      if(ix>=0&&iy>=0&&ix<GW&&iy<GH)mask[idx(ix,iy)]=1;
    };
    for(const sw of (swales||[])){
      const pts=sw&&sw.pts||[];
      for(let j=0;j<pts.length;j++){
        mark(pts[j][0],pts[j][1]);
        if(j===0)continue;
        const a=pts[j-1],b=pts[j];
        const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0]),Math.abs(b[1]-a[1]))*2));
        for(let k=1;k<steps;k++){
          const t=k/steps;mark(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t);
        }
      }
    }
    return mask;
  }
  function earthlineEDT1D15803(f,n){
    const v=new Int32Array(n),z=new Float64Array(n+1),d=new Float64Array(n);
    let k=0;v[0]=0;z[0]=-Infinity;z[1]=Infinity;
    for(let q=1;q<n;q++){
      let s;
      do{
        const p=v[k];
        s=((f[q]+q*q)-(f[p]+p*p))/(2*q-2*p);
        if(s<=z[k])k--;
      }while(s<=z[k]&&k>=0);
      k++;v[k]=q;z[k]=s;z[k+1]=Infinity;
    }
    k=0;
    for(let q=0;q<n;q++){
      while(z[k+1]<q)k++;
      const p=v[k];d[q]=(q-p)*(q-p)+f[p];
    }
    return d;
  }
  function earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask){
    const BIG=1e12,tmp=new Float64Array(N),out=new Float64Array(N);
    for(let y=0;y<GH;y++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)f[x]=corridorMask[idx(x,y)]?0:BIG;
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)tmp[idx(x,y)]=d[x];
    }
    for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)f[y]=tmp[idx(x,y)];
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)out[idx(x,y)]=Math.sqrt(d[y])*Math.max(.001,Number(M.cellM||1));
    }
    return out;
  }

  function earthlineComputeCoverage15803(diag,swales,maxS,chanThr){
    const corridorMask=earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales);
    let corridorPixels=0;for(const v of corridorMask)corridorPixels+=v?1:0;
    const thresholds=[
      {key:"within25ft",ft:25,m:25/3.280839895},
      {key:"within50ft",ft:50,m:50/3.280839895},
      {key:"within100ft",ft:100,m:100/3.280839895}
    ];
    const counts={within25ft:0,within50ft:0,within100ft:0};
    const dist=corridorPixels?earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask):null;
    let safe=0;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS||!Number.isFinite(Number(M.h[i])))continue;
      safe++;
      const z=earthlineCoverageZone15803(diag,x,y);
      const dm=dist?dist[i]:Infinity;
      for(const t of thresholds)if(dm<=t.m){counts[t.key]++;z.coverage[t.key]++;}
    }
    const pct={};
    for(const t of thresholds)pct[t.key]=safe?100*counts[t.key]/safe:0;
    for(const z of diag.zones){
      for(const t of thresholds)z.coverage[t.key+"Pct"]=z.safeCells?100*z.coverage[t.key]/z.safeCells:0;
    }
    diag.coverage={
      method:"exact Euclidean grid distance to rasterized accepted corridor centerlines",
      cellM:Number(M.cellM||0),safeCells:safe,corridorPixels,
      counts,pct,
      within25ftPct:pct.within25ft,
      within50ftPct:pct.within50ft,
      within100ftPct:pct.within100ft
    };
  }
  function earthlineFinalizeCoverageDiagnostic15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr,kept,checked){
    if(!diag)return null;
    diag.totals.accepted=kept.length;

    diag.termination.truncated=!!(
      diag.termination.candidateCapReached||
      diag.termination.finalCapReached||
      diag.termination.timeBudgetReached
    );
    const reasons=[];
    if(diag.termination.candidateCapReached)reasons.push("candidate-cap");
    if(diag.termination.finalCapReached)reasons.push("final-cap");
    if(diag.termination.timeBudgetReached)reasons.push("time-budget");
    if(!reasons.length)reasons.push(diag.termination.candidateStage||"exhausted");
    diag.termination.reason=reasons.join("+");
    earthlineFinalizeCandidateAccounting15840(diag);
    earthlineFinalizeSeedFates15840(diag);
    earthlineFinalizePreselectionFate15841(diag);
    earthlineMeasureContourCells15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr);
    earthlineComputeCoverage15803(diag,kept,maxS,chanThr);
    diag.mapFeatures=earthlineBuildBoundaryEvidence15803(diag);
    diag.runtimeBuild=String(window.EARTHLINE_BUILD||diag.runtimeBuild||"");
    diag.noBuildProvenance15843=diag.noBuildProvenance15843||null;
    diag.completedAt=new Date().toISOString();
    M.coverageDiagnostic15803=diag;
    return diag;
  }

async function computeRecharge(myGen=M.searchGen){
  let vh=0,hi=0,mo=0,lo=0,vl=0,totalSuit=0,tot=0;
  const top=[];
  const deferPropertyRechargeSelection16409=!!M.deferPropertyRechargeSelection16409;
  const propertyCandidates16409=deferPropertyRechargeSelection16409?[]:null;
  function addTop(s,i){
    if(!Number.isFinite(s))return;
    if(deferPropertyRechargeSelection16409){propertyCandidates16409.push([s,i]);return;}
    const item=[s,i];
    let j=0;while(j<top.length&&top[j][0]>=s)j++;
    top.splice(j,0,item);
    if(top.length>80)top.pop();
  }
  for(let y=0;y<GH;y++){
    for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);if(!M.inP[i])continue;
      const suitability=rechSuit(i);
      if(!Number.isFinite(suitability))continue;
      const s=clamp(suitability,0,1);
      totalSuit+=s;tot++;addTop(s,i);
      if(s>=0.54)vh++;else if(s>=0.47)hi++;else if(s>=0.40)mo++;else if(s>=0.33)lo++;else vl++;
    }
    if((y&7)===7){earthlineAssertActive15686(myGen);await earthlineCoopYield15686(myGen,"hydrology");}
  }
  if(!tot){
    M.donut=[];M.donutCenter=null;M.rechargeStats={analyzedCells:0,modeledPotentialPct:null,highVeryHighPct:null};
    M.rechZones=[];
    if(deferPropertyRechargeSelection16409){M.rechargeCandidates16409=[];M.rechargeTargetSelection16332=null;}
    earthlineUpdateRechargeSvg(false);return;
  }
  const counts=[vh,hi,mo,lo,vl];
  let p=counts.map(v=>Math.round(v/tot*100));
  const raw=counts.map(v=>v/tot*100);
  let delta=100-p.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
  let mi=0;for(let k=1;k<raw.length;k++)if(raw[k]>raw[mi])mi=k;
  p[mi]+=delta;
  const precipIn=(M.loc&&M.loc.precipIn)||30;
  const floor=Math.round(clamp((40-precipIn)/32,0,1)*45);
  let highVeryHigh=p[0]+p[1];
  if(highVeryHigh<floor){
    let need=floor-highVeryHigh;
    for(const gi of [4,3,2]){
      if(need<=0)break;
      const take=Math.min(Math.max(0,p[gi]),need);
      if(take>0){p[gi]-=take;p[1]+=take;need-=take;}
    }
    highVeryHigh=p[0]+p[1];
  }
  M.donut=[
    {n:"Very High",c:"#e53935",p:p[0]},
    {n:"High",c:"#fb8c00",p:p[1]},
    {n:"Moderate",c:"#fdd835",p:p[2]},
    {n:"Low",c:"#43a047",p:p[3]},
    {n:"Very Low",c:"#1e88e5",p:p[4]}
  ];
  const modeledPotentialPct=Math.round(totalSuit/tot*100);
  M.donutCenter=clamp(modeledPotentialPct,1,99);
  M.rechargeStats={analyzedCells:tot,modeledPotentialPct:M.donutCenter,highVeryHighPct:highVeryHigh};
  if(deferPropertyRechargeSelection16409){
    M.rechZones=[];
    M.rechargeCandidates16409=propertyCandidates16409;
    M.rechargeTargetSelection16332=null;
    return;
  }
  M.rechZones=[];
  const containmentScales16332=[1,.90,.80,.70,.60,.50,.42,.34,.28,.24];
  let rejectedNoBuild16332=0,rejectedContainment16332=0,rejectedSpacing16332=0;

  for(const [s,i] of top){
    if(M.rechZones.length>=5)break;
    const x=i%GW,y=(i/GW)|0;

    if((M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i])||(M.pond&&M.pond[i])){
      rejectedNoBuild16332++;continue;
    }

    const[ggx,ggy]=gradAt(x,y);
    const ang=Math.atan2(ggy,ggx)+Math.PI/2;
    const rawZone16332={
      gx:x,gy:y,rx:14+s*16,ry:7+s*6,a:ang,s,
      id:"R"+(M.rechZones.length+1)
    };

    let fittedScale16332=null;
    for(const scale16332 of containmentScales16332){
      if(
        typeof earthlineRechargeZoneFitsProperty16326==="function"&&
        earthlineRechargeZoneFitsProperty16326(rawZone16332,scale16332)
      ){
        fittedScale16332=scale16332;break;
      }
    }
    if(fittedScale16332==null){rejectedContainment16332++;continue;}

    let near=false;
    for(const z of M.rechZones){
      const minSep16332=Math.max(
        16,
        Math.min(24,(Number(z.rx||10)+Number(rawZone16332.rx||10))*.45)
      );
      if(Math.hypot(z.gx-x,z.gy-y)<minSep16332){near=true;break;}
    }
    if(near){rejectedSpacing16332++;continue;}

    M.rechZones.push(Object.assign({},rawZone16332,{
      rx:rawZone16332.rx*fittedScale16332,
      ry:rawZone16332.ry*fittedScale16332,
      propertyContainmentScale16332:fittedScale16332
    }));
  }

  M.rechargeTargetSelection16332={
    build:"EARTHLINE 16332",
    rankedCandidates:Number(top.length),
    selectedTargets:Number(M.rechZones.length),
    rejectedNoBuild:Number(rejectedNoBuild16332),
    rejectedContainment:Number(rejectedContainment16332),
    rejectedSpacing:Number(rejectedSpacing16332),
    suitabilityScoresChanged:false,
    at:new Date().toISOString()
  };
}
/* ---------------- bioswale prescription ---------------- */
// Sample the loaded satellite imagery to estimate tree canopy at a grid cell.
// Forest = dark, saturated green, high texture. Open ground (grass/soil/roof/dry)
// is lighter, less saturated. Returns 0 (open) .. 1 (dense canopy).
let _canopyData=null,_canopyW=0,_canopyH=0,_impervData=null,_satRgbaData=null;
function buildCanopyField(){
  _canopyData=null;_impervData=null;_satRgbaData=null;
  if(!(M.realMode&&M.satCanvas))return;          // only meaningful on real imagery
  try{
    const sc=M.satCanvas, sw=sc.width, sh=sc.height;
    const id=sc.getContext("2d").getImageData(0,0,sw,sh).data;
    _satRgbaData=id;
    _canopyW=sw;_canopyH=sh;_canopyData=new Float32Array(sw*sh);
    _impervData=new Float32Array(sw*sh);
    for(let p=0,q=0;p<id.length;p+=4,q++){
      const r=id[p],g=id[p+1],b=id[p+2];
      // Forest reads as dark, green-dominant. Open ground (grass/dry/soil/roof/road)
      // is brighter and/or red-leaning. Build a robust 0..1 canopy score.
      const green=g-(r+b)/2;                 // green dominance
      const lum=(r+g+b)/3;
      // TRUE forest canopy = dark AND strongly green together. Open sage/grass is
      // mid-tone and only weakly green, and must NOT register as forest.
      let c=0;
      const isDark=lum<95;                    // genuinely shaded canopy
      const strongGreen=green>22;             // clearly green-dominant (not pale sage)
      if(isDark && green>12) c=0.55+Math.min(0.4,(green-12)/40);  // dark + green = tree
      else if(strongGreen && lum<140) c=0.45+Math.min(0.4,(green-22)/40); // lush green
      else c=0;                                // bright, pale, dry, or red = open ground
      if(lum>160)c=0;                          // anything bright = open, hard rule
      if(r>=g)c=0;                             // red/tan-leaning = dry open ground
      _canopyData[q]=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,c));
      // IMPERVIOUS (roof/pavement/concrete/asphalt): man-made surfaces are low-saturation
      // (grey/blue-grey) and NOT green vegetation, across a wide brightness range — bright
      // concrete, mid pavement, AND dark shingle/asphalt roofs.
      const mx=Math.max(r,g,b), mn=Math.min(r,g,b);
      const sat=mx?(mx-mn)/mx:0;              // colour saturation 0..1
      let imp=0;
      if(green<14 && c<0.25){                 // exclude vegetation/canopy
        // Man-made surfaces: low-to-moderate saturation greys across brightness. We bias
        // toward catching roofs even at some risk of flagging a little bare ground, because
        // a swale stopping short of a roof is FAR safer than one drawn across a house.
        if(lum>160 && sat<0.20) imp=0.75;                              // bright concrete/roof
        else if(lum>=55 && lum<=160 && sat<0.18) imp=0.62;            // grey/warm-grey roof
        else if(b>=r && b>g-2 && lum>70 && sat<0.32) imp=0.6;         // blue-grey metal roof
        else if(lum>=50 && lum<=120 && sat<0.22) imp=0.55;           // dark shingle/asphalt
      }
      _impervData[q]=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,imp));
    }
  }catch(e){_canopyData=null;_impervData=null;_satRgbaData=null;}   // CORS taint etc -> skip gracefully
}
// canopy at grid cell (gx,gy) by mapping grid -> satCanvas pixel space
function canopyAt(gx,gy){
  if(!_canopyData)return 0;
  const px=Math.round((gx/GW)*_canopyW), py=Math.round((gy/GH)*_canopyH);
  let sum=0,nn=0;
  for(let oy=-2;oy<=2;oy++)for(let ox=-2;ox<=2;ox++){
    const x=px+ox,y=py+oy;
    if(x<0||y<0||x>=_canopyW||y>=_canopyH)continue;
    sum+=_canopyData[y*_canopyW+x];nn++;
  }
  return nn?sum/nn:0;
}
// impervious (hard-surface) score at grid cell, same mapping as canopyAt
function impervAt(gx,gy){
  if(!_impervData)return 0;
  const pxr=Math.round((gx/GW)*_canopyW), pyr=Math.round((gy/GH)*_canopyH);
  let sum=0,nn=0;
  for(let oy=-2;oy<=2;oy++)for(let ox=-2;ox<=2;ox++){
    const x=pxr+ox,y=pyr+oy;
    if(x<0||y<0||x>=_canopyW||y>=_canopyH)continue;
    sum+=_impervData[y*_canopyW+x];nn++;
  }
  return nn?sum/nn:0;
}
// mean canopy along a traced corridor path
function pathCanopy(pts){
  if(!_canopyData||!pts.length)return 0;
  let sum=0;for(const p of pts)sum+=canopyAt(p[0],p[1]);
  return sum/pts.length;
}
// median slope (rise/run fraction) over in-parcel cells — used to cap swale spacing
function medianSlopeInParcel(){
  const vals=[];
  for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y+=2)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x+=2){
    const i=idx(x,y);if(!M.inP[i])continue;
    vals.push(slopePct(x,y)/100);
  }
  if(!vals.length)return 0.05;
  vals.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  return vals[Math.floor(vals.length/2)];
}
function earthlineDensityTargetM(){
  // BUILD-CHECK-15760: approved 15–25 ft centerline spacing, defaulting to 20 ft.
  // This is a ground-distance target, never a pixel, zoom, frame-height, or swale-count proxy.
  const requested=Number(M.targetSwaleSpacingM||6.096);
  return clamp(requested,4.572,7.620);
}

function earthlineImpervNear(cx,cy,r){
  let mx=0;
  for(let oy=-r;oy<=r;oy++)for(let ox=-r;ox<=r;ox++){
    const x=cx+ox,y=cy+oy;
    if(x<0||y<0||x>=GW||y>=GH)continue;
    const v=impervAt(x,y);if(v>mx)mx=v;
  }
  return mx;
}

function earthlineCellCrossesLevel(cx,cy,L){
  if(cx<0||cy<0||cx>=GW-1||cy>=GH-1)return false;
  const h00=M.h[idx(cx,cy)], h10=M.h[idx(cx+1,cy)],
        h01=M.h[idx(cx,cy+1)], h11=M.h[idx(cx+1,cy+1)];
  const mn=Math.min(h00,h10,h01,h11), mx=Math.max(h00,h10,h01,h11);
  return L>=mn && L<=mx && mx-mn>1e-7;
}

let earthlineMajorChannelUnsafe16288=null,earthlineMajorChannelUnsafeKey16288="";
function earthlineMajorChannelUnsafeAt16288(cx,cy){
  const threshold=streamThreshold()*3.2;
  const radius=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(4/Math.max(0.5,M.cellM||1)));
  const key=String(M.searchGen||0)+"|"+String(M.maxAcc||0)+"|"+String(M.rain||0)+"|"+String(M.cellM||0)+"|"+threshold+"|"+radius;
  if(!earthlineMajorChannelUnsafe16288||earthlineMajorChannelUnsafe16288.length!==N||earthlineMajorChannelUnsafeKey16288!==key){
    const unsafe=new Uint8Array(N);
    if(M.acc&&M.acc.length===N){
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
        if(M.acc[idx(x,y)]<threshold)continue;
        for(let oy=-radius;oy<=radius;oy++)for(let ox=-radius;ox<=radius;ox++){
          const nx=x+ox,ny=y+oy;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=GW||ny>=GH)continue;unsafe[idx(nx,ny)]=1;
        }
      }
    }
    earthlineMajorChannelUnsafe16288=unsafe;earthlineMajorChannelUnsafeKey16288=key;
  }
  return !!earthlineMajorChannelUnsafe16288[idx(cx,cy)];
}
function earthlineSafeCell(cx,cy,maxS){
  if(cx<1||cy<1||cx>=GW-1||cy>=GH-1)return false;
  const i=idx(cx,cy);
  if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i])return false;
  if(slopePct(cx,cy)>maxS)return false;
  const frozen=!!(M.prescriptionNoBuildRun15843&&M.prescriptionNoBuildRun15843.active&&Number(M.prescriptionNoBuildRun15843.searchGen)===Number(M.searchGen)&&M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask.length===N);
  if(!frozen)earthlineBuildNoBuildMask();
  if(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i])return false;
  if(earthlineMajorChannelUnsafeAt16288(cx,cy))return false;
  return true;
}

function earthlineSafeCellCached16320(cx,cy,maxS){
  if(cx<1||cy<1||cx>=GW-1||cy>=GH-1)return false;
  const c=M&&M.earthlineTraceSafeCache16320;
  if(c&&Number(c.searchGen)===Number(M.searchGen)&&Number(c.maxS)===Number(maxS)&&
     c.mask&&c.mask.length===N&&c.known&&c.known.length===N){
    const i=idx(cx,cy);
    if(c.known[i]){c.hits=(c.hits||0)+1;return !!c.mask[i];}
    const value=!!earthlineSafeCell(cx,cy,maxS);
    c.known[i]=1;c.mask[i]=value?1:0;c.misses=(c.misses||0)+1;
    if(value)c.safeCells=(c.safeCells||0)+1;
    return value;
  }
  return earthlineSafeCell(cx,cy,maxS);
}
function earthlineNearestContourCell(sx,sy,L,maxS){
  const x0=Math.round(sx),y0=Math.round(sy);
  let best=null,bestD=Infinity;
  for(let r=0;r<=8;r++){
    for(let oy=-r;oy<=r;oy++)for(let ox=-r;ox<=r;ox++){
      if(Math.max(Math.abs(ox),Math.abs(oy))!==r)continue;
      const x=x0+ox,y=y0+oy;
      if(!earthlineSafeCellCached16320(x,y,maxS))continue;
      if(!earthlineCellCrossesLevel(x,y,L))continue;
      const d=ox*ox+oy*oy;
      if(d<bestD){bestD=d;best=[x,y];}
    }
    if(best)return best;
  }
  return null;
}

function earthlineClaimPath(mask,pts,r){
  for(const [px,py] of pts){
    const gx=Math.round(px),gy=Math.round(py);
    for(let oy=-r;oy<=r;oy++)for(let ox=-r;ox<=r;ox++){
      const nx=gx+ox,ny=gy+oy;
      if(nx>=0&&ny>=0&&nx<GW&&ny<GH)mask[idx(nx,ny)]=1;
    }
  }
}

function earthlinePathOverlap(mask,pts){
  if(!pts.length)return 1;
  let hit=0;
  for(const [px,py] of pts){
    const gx=Math.round(px),gy=Math.round(py);
    if(gx>=0&&gy>=0&&gx<GW&&gy<GH&&mask[idx(gx,gy)])hit++;
  }
  return hit/pts.length;
}

function earthlineCleanPath(pts){
  if(!pts||pts.length<3)return pts||[];
  const out=[];
  let last=null;
  for(const p of pts){
    if(!last||Math.hypot(p[0]-last[0],p[1]-last[1])>0.08){out.push(p);last=p;}
  }
  if(out.length<3)return out;
  const sm=[out[0]];
  for(let i=1;i<out.length-1;i++){
    const p0=out[i-1],p=out[i],p1=out[i+1];
    sm.push([(p0[0]+2*p[0]+p1[0])/4,(p0[1]+2*p[1]+p1[1])/4]);
  }
  sm.push(out[out.length-1]);
  return sm;
}


// Engineering setback assumptions for screening only. These are intentionally conservative,
// because an infiltration swale too close to a foundation, driveway, or road can create
// seepage, frost-heave, shoulder, and access issues. Final construction still needs local
// engineering, utilities, septic, well, and right-of-way review.
const EARTHLINE_SETBACK_M={
  structure:10.0,      // ~33 ft screening buffer from confirmed buildings/foundations
  pavedEdge:7.0,       // ~23 ft planning buffer from confirmed road/driveway/pavement edge
  minimum:3.5          // never less than ~11.5 ft from confirmed hard surface
};







function earthlineSegmentLengthM(pts){
  if(!pts||pts.length<2)return 0;
  let d=0;
  for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)d+=Math.hypot(pts[i][0]-pts[i-1][0],pts[i][1]-pts[i-1][1])*M.cellM;
  return d;
}

/* BUILD-CHECK-15768
   Minimum corridor length is a physical screening threshold, not an uncapped
   multiple of regional raster-cell size. This keeps property and regional
   analyses on the same real-world rule without requiring hundreds of metres
   of uninterrupted contour at coarse tile resolution. */
function earthlineMinimumSwaleLengthM15768(){
  const cell=Math.max(0.25,Number(M.cellM||1));
  return Math.max(12,Math.min(40,cell*4));
}

function earthlineSplitUsableSegments(pts,maxS){
  // Split a contour wherever it touches a no-build setback. This avoids drawing across or
  // immediately beside roofs, roads, and driveways while preserving longer buildable contours.
  const segs=[];
  let cur=[];
  const minM=earthlineMinimumSwaleLengthM15768(); // physical 12–40 m corridor-length threshold
  let prev=null;
  for(const p of (pts||[])){
    // Check the actual line between vertices, not only the vertices. This prevents a smoothed
    // swale from visually crossing a narrow driveway/roof between sampled points.
    let ok=earthlinePathPointUsable(p[0],p[1],maxS);
    if(ok&&prev){
      const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(p[0]-prev[0],p[1]-prev[1])*2));
      for(let k=1;k<steps;k++){
        const t=k/steps;
        if(!earthlinePathPointUsable(prev[0]+(p[0]-prev[0])*t,prev[1]+(p[1]-prev[1])*t,maxS)){ok=false;break;}
      }
    }
    if(ok)cur.push(p);
    else{
      if(earthlineSegmentLengthM(cur)>=minM)segs.push(earthlineCleanPath(cur));
      cur=[];
    }
    prev=p;
  }
  if(earthlineSegmentLengthM(cur)>=minM)segs.push(earthlineCleanPath(cur));
  segs.sort((a,b)=>earthlineSegmentLengthM(b)-earthlineSegmentLengthM(a));
  return segs;
}


function earthlineVecUnit(ax,ay,bx,by){
  const dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,m=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
  return [dx/m,dy/m];
}
function earthlineDot2(a,b){return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1];}
function earthlineBridgeUsable(a,b,maxS){
  const gapCells=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
  const steps=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(gapCells*2.5));
  for(let k=1;k<steps;k++){
    const t=k/steps;
    if(!earthlinePathPointUsable(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t,maxS))return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function earthlineJoinPathsIfCompatible(a,b,maxS,maxGapM){
  if(!a||!b||a.length<2||b.length<2)return null;
  const variants=[
    [a,b],
    [a,b.slice().reverse()],
    [a.slice().reverse(),b],
    [a.slice().reverse(),b.slice().reverse()]
  ];
  let best=null;
  for(const pair of variants){
    const pa=pair[0],pb=pair[1],ae=pa[pa.length-1],bs=pb[0];
    const gapM=Math.hypot(bs[0]-ae[0],bs[1]-ae[1])*M.cellM;
    if(gapM>maxGapM)continue;
    const ta=earthlineVecUnit(pa[pa.length-2][0],pa[pa.length-2][1],ae[0],ae[1]);
    const tb=earthlineVecUnit(bs[0],bs[1],pb[1][0],pb[1][1]);
    const bridge=earthlineVecUnit(ae[0],ae[1],bs[0],bs[1]);
    // A contour can curve, but a join should continue generally forward rather than
    // doubling back into a hook or connecting unrelated parallel lines.
    // 15624: a join must continue in the same general direction. The previous permissive
    // threshold allowed hook and near-90-degree connections that did not resemble a level swale.
    const minForward=gapM<=Math.max(2.5,M.cellM*2.5)?0.48:0.68;
    const aligned=earthlineDot2(ta,bridge)>minForward&&
      earthlineDot2(bridge,tb)>minForward&&earthlineDot2(ta,tb)>0.50;
    if(!aligned||!earthlineBridgeUsable(ae,bs,maxS))continue;
    const gapCells=Math.hypot(bs[0]-ae[0],bs[1]-ae[1]);
    const n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(gapCells));
    const bridgePts=[];
    for(let k=1;k<n;k++){
      const t=k/n;bridgePts.push([ae[0]+(bs[0]-ae[0])*t,ae[1]+(bs[1]-ae[1])*t]);
    }
    const joined=earthlineCleanPath(pa.concat(bridgePts,pb));
    const rank=gapM+(1-earthlineDot2(ta,tb))*2;
    if(!best||rank<best.rank)best={pts:joined,rank};
  }
  return best?best.pts:null;
}
function earthlineMergeCompatibleSwales(swales,chanThr,maxS,totalBands,targetM){
  const pool=(swales||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.band-b.band)||(b.lenM-a.lenM));
  const used=new Uint8Array(pool.length),out=[];
  const maxGapM=Math.min(8,Math.max(4,targetM*0.65));
  for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){
    if(used[i])continue;
    used[i]=1;
    let current=pool[i],changed=true,passes=0;
    while(changed&&passes++<6){
      changed=false;let bestJ=-1,bestPts=null,bestLen=0;
      for(let j=0;j<pool.length;j++){
        if(used[j]||pool[j].band!==current.band)continue;
        const joined=earthlineJoinPathsIfCompatible(current.pts,pool[j].pts,maxS,maxGapM);
        if(!joined)continue;
        const len=earthlineSegmentLengthM(joined);
        if(len>bestLen){bestLen=len;bestJ=j;bestPts=joined;}
      }
      if(bestJ>=0){
        const other=pool[bestJ];
        const rebuilt=earthlineSwaleFromPts(bestPts,current.band,chanThr,current.isSite||other.isSite,maxS,totalBands);
        if(rebuilt){
          rebuilt.merged=true;
          current=rebuilt;used[bestJ]=1;changed=true;
        }
      }
    }
    out.push(current);
  }
  return out;
}

function earthlineSwaleGeometryAcceptable(pts,lenM){
  if(!pts||pts.length<3||lenM<12)return false;
  const a=pts[0],b=pts[pts.length-1],gapM=Math.hypot(a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1])*M.cellM;
  let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
  for(const p of pts){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minY=Math.min(minY,p[1]);maxY=Math.max(maxY,p[1]);}
  const diagM=Math.hypot(maxX-minX,maxY-minY)*M.cellM;
  const loopish=gapM<Math.max(8,lenM*0.12);
  // Suppress small closed rings and compact bullseyes caused by neighborhood/DEM artifacts.
  if(loopish&&(lenM<240||lenM/Math.max(1,diagM)>3.2))return false;
  if(lenM/Math.max(1,diagM)>6)return false;
  return true;
}

let earthlineCaptureVisitMark16455=new Uint32Array(N);
let earthlineCaptureVisitToken16455=0;
function earthlineCaptureToken16455(){
  earthlineCaptureVisitToken16455=(earthlineCaptureVisitToken16455+1)>>>0;
  if(earthlineCaptureVisitToken16455===0){earthlineCaptureVisitMark16455.fill(0);earthlineCaptureVisitToken16455=1;}
  return earthlineCaptureVisitToken16455;
}
function earthlineSwaleFromPts(pts,bandIndex,chanThr,isSite,maxS,totalBands){
  if(!pts||pts.length<2)return null;
  let canopySum=0,slopeSum=0,capCells=0,breakSum=0,impUpSum=0,impOnSum=0;
  /* EARTHLINE 16455 — exact performance repair only. The former per-candidate Set
     represented whether each grid cell had already contributed to capCells. A reusable
     Uint32 generation marker represents the identical membership state without allocating
     and hashing thousands of numbers for every candidate. Capture geometry, accumulation
     values, channel threshold, scoring and candidate ordering are unchanged. */
  const captureToken16455=earthlineCaptureToken16455();

  for(const [px,py] of pts){
    const gx=Math.round(px),gy=Math.round(py);
    if(gx<0||gy<0||gx>=GW||gy>=GH)continue;

    canopySum+=canopyAt(gx,gy);
    impOnSum+=impervAt(gx,gy);

    const spHere=slopePct(gx,gy);
    slopeSum+=spHere;

    const [ggx,ggy]=gradAt(gx,gy);
    const gm=Math.hypot(ggx,ggy)||1;

    const ux=Math.round(gx+(ggx/gm)*3),uy=Math.round(gy+(ggy/gm)*3);
    if(ux>=0&&uy>=0&&ux<GW&&uy<GH){
      const spUp=slopePct(ux,uy);
      if(spUp-spHere>3)breakSum++;
    }

    const ix=Math.round(gx+(ggx/gm)*6),iy=Math.round(gy+(ggy/gm)*6);
    if(ix>=0&&iy>=0&&ix<GW&&iy<GH)impUpSum+=impervAt(ix,iy);

    for(let oy=-2;oy<=2;oy++)for(let ox=-2;ox<=2;ox++){
      const nx=gx+ox,ny=gy+oy;
      if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=GW||ny>=GH)continue;
      const ni=idx(nx,ny);
      if(earthlineCaptureVisitMark16455[ni]!==captureToken16455){
        earthlineCaptureVisitMark16455[ni]=captureToken16455;
        if(M.acc[ni]<chanThr)capCells+=M.acc[ni];
      }
    }
  }

  const n=Math.max(1,pts.length);
  const canopy=canopySum/n;
  const forested=canopy>0.40;
  const avgSlope=slopeSum/n;
  if(avgSlope>maxS)return null;

  const impOn=impOnSum/n;
  const impUp=impUpSum/n;
  const lenM=earthlineSegmentLengthM(pts);
  if(!earthlineSwaleGeometryAcceptable(pts,lenM))return null;
  // Prefer substantial, buildable segments over tiny contour fragments.
  if(!isSite && lenM<earthlineMinimumSwaleLengthM15768())return null;

  let slopeScore;
  if(avgSlope<1.5)slopeScore=0.84;
  else if(avgSlope<=8)slopeScore=1.0;
  else if(avgSlope<=18)slopeScore=1.0-(avgSlope-8)/10*0.45;
  else slopeScore=Math.max(0.20,1.0-(avgSlope-18)/(maxS-18)*0.80);

  const breakScore=Math.min(1,breakSum/Math.max(1,pts.length*0.25));
  const catchScore=Math.min(1,capCells/4000);
  const highScore=1-(bandIndex/Math.max(1,totalBands));
  const lenScore=Math.min(1,lenM/80);
  const forestPenalty=forested?0.84:1.0;
  const percScore=soilPercScore(pts);
  const impOnPenalty=1-Math.min(0.82,impOn*1.5);
  const impRunoffScore=Math.min(1,impUp*3.5);
  const siteBonus=isSite?0.15:0;

  const score=(slopeScore*0.30 + breakScore*0.18 + catchScore*0.19 + highScore*0.12
              + impRunoffScore*0.09 + lenScore*0.08 + siteBonus) * forestPenalty * impOnPenalty;

  return {
    pts,capCells,lenM,slope:avgSlope,canopy,forested,kind:"contour",band:bandIndex,score,isSite:!!isSite,
    slopeScore,breakScore,catchScore,percScore,impOn,impUp,id:""
  };
}


function traceLevelContour(sx,sy,L,maxS,dropH){
  const start=earthlineNearestContourCell(sx,sy,L,maxS);
  if(!start)return [];

  const cross=(ha,hb)=>{
    const d=hb-ha;
    if(Math.abs(d)<1e-9)return 0.5;
    return clamp((L-ha)/d,0,1);
  };

  const edgesFor=(cx,cy)=>{
    if(cx<0||cy<0||cx>=GW-1||cy>=GH-1)return [];
    const h00=M.h[idx(cx,cy)],h10=M.h[idx(cx+1,cy)],
          h01=M.h[idx(cx,cy+1)],h11=M.h[idx(cx+1,cy+1)];
    const e=[];
    if((h00<=L)!==(h10<=L))e.push([0,cx+cross(h00,h10),cy]);
    if((h10<=L)!==(h11<=L))e.push([1,cx+1,cy+cross(h10,h11)]);
    if((h01<=L)!==(h11<=L))e.push([2,cx+cross(h01,h11),cy+1]);
    if((h00<=L)!==(h01<=L))e.push([3,cx,cy+cross(h00,h01)]);
    return e;
  };

  const walk=(firstPick)=>{
    const pts=[];
    let cx=start[0],cy=start[1],prevEdge=-1;
    const visited=new Set();

    for(let step=0;step<1600;step++){
      if(!earthlineSafeCell(cx,cy,maxS))break;

      const key=cx+","+cy+","+prevEdge;
      if(visited.has(key))break;
      visited.add(key);

      const edges=edgesFor(cx,cy);
      if(edges.length===0)break;

      let candidates=prevEdge>=0?edges.filter(e=>e[0]!==prevEdge):edges;
      if(!candidates.length)candidates=edges;

      const pick=(step===0)?candidates[firstPick%candidates.length]:candidates[0];
      pts.push([pick[1],pick[2]]);

      let nx=cx,ny=cy,entered=-1;
      if(pick[0]===0){ny=cy-1;entered=2;}
      else if(pick[0]===1){nx=cx+1;entered=3;}
      else if(pick[0]===2){ny=cy+1;entered=0;}
      else{nx=cx-1;entered=1;}

      cx=nx;cy=ny;prevEdge=entered;
    }

    return pts;
  };

  const a=walk(0),b=walk(1);
  const merged=a.slice().reverse().concat(b);
  if(merged.length<3)return [];

  return earthlineCleanPath(merged);
}

function soilPercScore(pts){
  if(!_soilData)return 0.6;                      // neutral when soil layer not loaded
  let sum=0,nn=0;
  for(const[px,py] of pts){
    const v=soilPercAt(Math.round(px),Math.round(py));
    if(v>=0){sum+=v;nn++;}
  }
  return nn?sum/nn:0.6;
}

function traceContour(sx,sy,chanThr,maxS){
  const half=(dirSign)=>{
    const pts=[];
    let x=sx,y=sy,px=0,pyv=0;
    let crossed=0;
    for(let step=0;step<90;step++){
      const[gx,gy]=gradAt(Math.round(x),Math.round(y));
      const mag=Math.hypot(gx,gy);
      if(mag<1e-7)break;
      let dx=-gy/mag*dirSign*0.85,dy=gx/mag*dirSign*0.85; // smaller step = smoother
      if(step>0&&(dx*px+dy*pyv)<0){dx=-dx;dy=-dy;}
      px=dx;pyv=dy;
      x+=dx;y+=dy;
      const gxi=Math.round(x),gyi=Math.round(y);
      if(gxi<2||gyi<2||gxi>=GW-2||gyi>=GH-2)break;
      const i=idx(gxi,gyi);
      if(!M.inP[i])break;
      const sp=slopePct(gxi,gyi);
      if(sp>maxS+3)break;            // relaxed slope ceiling for noisy real DEM
      if(M.acc[i]>=chanThr){          // allow crossing a minor channel, stop at a big one
        if(++crossed>3)break;
      }
      pts.push([x,y]);
    }
    return pts;
  };
  const a=half(1),b=half(-1);
  b.reverse();
  b.push([sx,sy]);
  return b.concat(a);
}

/* ---------------- USGS Principal Aquifers (live GIS overlay) ---------------- */
function currentMapBBox(){
  const halfW=GW*M.cellM/2, halfH=GH*M.cellM/2;
  const dLat=halfH/111320;
  const dLng=halfW/(111320*Math.cos(M.centerLat*Math.PI/180));
  return {minLng:M.centerLng-dLng,maxLng:M.centerLng+dLng,minLat:M.centerLat-dLat,maxLat:M.centerLat+dLat};
}
function lngLatToGrid(lng,lat){
  const dxM=(lng-M.centerLng)*(111320*Math.cos(M.centerLat*Math.PI/180));
  const dyM=(lat-M.centerLat)*111320;
  return [GW/2+dxM/M.cellM, GH/2-dyM/M.cellM];
}
// ===== NRCS SSURGO soil perc data (real, no key) =====
// Hydrologic Soil Group -> perc suitability for swales (0..1). Mid perc is best:
// A (sandy, very fast) drains too fast to hold water; D (clay, very slow) won't infiltrate.
let _soilData=null,_soilW=0,_soilH=0;            // rasterized perc grid over the analysis window
const HSG_PERC={A:0.55,B:0.95,C:0.80,D:0.40};    // B/C loams = ideal swale ground
function soilPercAt(gx,gy){
  if(!_soilData)return -1;
  if(gx<0||gy<0||gx>=GW||gy>=GH)return -1;
  const sx=Math.round((gx/GW)*_soilW), sy=Math.round((gy/GH)*_soilH);
  if(sx<0||sy<0||sx>=_soilW||sy>=_soilH)return -1;
  const v=_soilData[sy*_soilW+sx];
  return v>0?v:-1;                                // 0 = no data
}
async function fetchSSURGOSoils(){
  if(M.ssurgoLoading)return;
  if(earthlineLargeAreaMode()){
    _soilData=null;
    toast("NRCS soil detail loads at property/county zoom — regional mode stays fast.");
    render();
    return;
  }
  const bb=currentMapBBox();
  M.ssurgoLoading=true;
  toast("Loading NRCS soil data\u2026");
  try{
    // NRCS Soil Data Access — spatial query returns mapunit polygons; we also pull the
    // dominant hydrologic group per mapunit. Use the SDA "post rest" tabular+spatial API.
    const aoi=bb.minLng+" "+bb.minLat+","+bb.maxLng+" "+bb.maxLat;
    const q="~DeclareGeometry(@aoi geometry)~ "
      +"select mukey, mupolygongeo.STAsText() as geom from mupolygon "
      +"where mupolygongeo.STIntersects(geometry::STGeomFromText("
      +"'POLYGON(("+bb.minLng+" "+bb.minLat+","+bb.maxLng+" "+bb.minLat+","
      +bb.maxLng+" "+bb.maxLat+","+bb.minLng+" "+bb.maxLat+","+bb.minLng+" "+bb.minLat+"))',4326))=1";
    const body={query:q,format:"JSON+COLUMNNAME"};
    const r=await fetch("https://sdmdataaccess.nrcs.usda.gov/Tabular/post.rest",{
      method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},body:JSON.stringify(body)});
    if(!r.ok)throw new Error("SDA HTTP "+r.status);
    const j=await r.json();
    const rows=(j.Table||[]).slice(1);            // first row is column names
    // fetch hydrologic group per mukey (dominant condition)
    const mukeys=[...new Set(rows.map(rw=>rw[0]))];
    const hsgMap=await fetchHSG(mukeys);
    rasterizeSoils(rows,hsgMap);
    M.ssurgoBox=bb;
    toast(_soilData?("NRCS soils loaded ("+mukeys.length+" map units)"):"NRCS: no soil polygons here");
  }catch(err){
    toast("NRCS soil fetch failed \u2014 check connection");
    _soilData=null;
  }finally{
    M.ssurgoLoading=false;render();
  }
}
async function fetchHSG(mukeys){
  const map={};
  if(!mukeys.length)return map;
  try{
    const list=mukeys.join(",");
    const q="select mu.mukey, c.hydgrp from mapunit mu "
      +"join component c on c.mukey=mu.mukey "
      +"where mu.mukey in ("+list+") and c.majcompflag='Yes'";
    const r=await fetch("https://sdmdataaccess.nrcs.usda.gov/Tabular/post.rest",{
      method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},
      body:JSON.stringify({query:q,format:"JSON"})});
    if(r.ok){
      const j=await r.json();
      (j.Table||[]).forEach(row=>{const mk=row[0],hg=(row[1]||"").charAt(0);if(hg&&!map[mk])map[mk]=hg;});
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return map;
}
function rasterizeSoils(rows,hsgMap){
  // burn mapunit polygons into a perc grid using an offscreen canvas
  _soilW=GW;_soilH=GH;
  const cv=document.createElement("canvas");cv.width=GW;cv.height=GH;
  const g=cv.getContext("2d");
  g.clearRect(0,0,GW,GH);
  let any=false;
  rows.forEach(rw=>{
    const mukey=rw[0], wkt=rw[1];
    if(!wkt)return;
    const hsg=hsgMap[mukey]||"C";
    const perc=HSG_PERC[hsg]!=null?HSG_PERC[hsg]:0.7;
    // encode perc 0..1 into the red channel 1..255 (0 reserved = no data)
    const enc=Math.max(1,Math.round(perc*255));
    g.fillStyle="rgb("+enc+",0,0)";
    parseWKTPolygons(wkt).forEach(ring=>{
      if(ring.length<3)return;any=true;
      g.beginPath();
      ring.forEach((c,i)=>{const[gx,gy]=lngLatToGrid(c[0],c[1]);i?g.lineTo(gx,gy):g.moveTo(gx,gy);});
      g.closePath();g.fill();
    });
  });
  if(!any){_soilData=null;return;}
  const im=g.getImageData(0,0,GW,GH).data;
  _soilData=new Float32Array(GW*GH);
  for(let i=0,q=0;i<im.length;i+=4,q++) _soilData[q]=im[i]/255;   // decode perc back to 0..1
}
function parseWKTPolygons(wkt){
  // minimal WKT POLYGON / MULTIPOLYGON ring extractor -> array of [ [lng,lat], ... ]
  const rings=[];
  const re=/\(([-0-9.,\s]+)\)/g;let m;
  while((m=re.exec(wkt))){
    const pts=m[1].trim().split(",").map(pr=>{
      const xy=pr.trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
      return [xy[0],xy[1]];
    }).filter(p=>p.length===2&&!isNaN(p[0])&&!isNaN(p[1]));
    if(pts.length>=3)rings.push(pts);
  }
  return rings;
}
// Rock-type -> relative permeability/recharge value (per USGS Ground Water Atlas classes).
// Sand & gravel and karst carbonate transmit water fastest; igneous/metamorphic slowest.
function aquiferPermFromName(name){
  const n=(name||"").toLowerCase();
  if(n.includes("sand and gravel")||n.includes("sand & gravel")||n.includes("basin-fill")||n.includes("allu",0))return 1.0;
  if(n.includes("carbonate")||n.includes("karst")||n.includes("limestone")||n.includes("dolomite"))return 0.92;
  if(n.includes("semiconsolidated")||n.includes("semi-consolidated"))return 0.8;
  if(n.includes("sandstone"))return 0.72;
  if(n.includes("basalt")||n.includes("volcanic"))return 0.6;
  if(n.includes("igneous")||n.includes("metamorphic")||n.includes("crystalline"))return 0.4;
  return 0.7;   // unknown principal aquifer = moderately permeable by default
}
function earthlineAquiferGeoJSON(){
  const features=[];
  (M.usgsAquifers||[]).forEach((poly,i)=>{
    const ring=(poly.lngLatPts||[]).filter(c=>Array.isArray(c)&&Number.isFinite(c[0])&&Number.isFinite(c[1]));
    if(ring.length<3)return;
    const closed=(ring[0][0]===ring[ring.length-1][0]&&ring[0][1]===ring[ring.length-1][1])?ring:ring.concat([ring[0]]);
    features.push({type:"Feature",id:"aq-"+i,properties:{
      featureKind:"aquifer-polygon",
      name:poly.name||"Mapped aquifer",
      source:poly.source||"Official aquifer data",
      aquiferType:poly.aquiferType||"aquifer",
      evidenceScale:poly.evidenceScale||""
    },geometry:{type:"Polygon",coordinates:[closed]}});
  });
  (M.vtGroundwaterWells16398||[]).forEach((w,i)=>{
    const lng=Number(w.lng),lat=Number(w.lat);if(!Number.isFinite(lng)||!Number.isFinite(lat))return;
    features.push({type:"Feature",id:"well-"+i,properties:{
      featureKind:"well",name:"Groundwater well",aquiferType:"vt-well-observation",
      evidence:String(w.evidence||"GROUNDWATER WELL"),distanceM:Number(w.distanceM||0),
      wellType:String(w.wellType||""),yieldGPM:Number.isFinite(Number(w.yieldGPM))?Number(w.yieldGPM):null,
      staticWaterLevel:Number.isFinite(Number(w.staticWaterLevel))?Number(w.staticWaterLevel):null,
      overBurdenThickness:Number.isFinite(Number(w.overBurdenThickness))?Number(w.overBurdenThickness):null,
      source:"VT ANR Private Wells"
    },geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:[lng,lat]}});
  });
  const summary=M.vtGroundwaterSummaryPoint16398;
  if(summary&&Number.isFinite(Number(summary.lng))&&Number.isFinite(Number(summary.lat))&&summary.label){
    features.push({type:"Feature",id:"vt-groundwater-summary",properties:{
      featureKind:"groundwater-summary",name:String(summary.label),aquiferType:"vt-groundwater-summary",
      source:"VT ANR groundwater evidence"
    },geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:[Number(summary.lng),Number(summary.lat)]}});
  }
  return {type:"FeatureCollection",features};
}
function earthlineApplyAquiferLayerStyle(){
  if(!earthlineMapActive()||!earthlineMap.getStyle())return false;
  try{
    // Re-apply paint every sync. This prevents an older layer instance or a style reload
    // from silently keeping stale, faint styling.
    const paint={
      fill:{
        "fill-color":"#ff7600",
        "fill-opacity":["interpolate",["linear"],["zoom"],5,0.86,10,0.82,13,0.76,16,0.68,19,0.60],
        "fill-outline-color":"rgba(45,15,0,1)",
        "fill-antialias":true
      },
      outer:{
        "line-color":"rgba(30,10,0,1)",
        "line-width":["interpolate",["linear"],["zoom"],5,22,10,20,13,17,16,13,19,10],
        "line-opacity":1,
        "line-blur":["interpolate",["linear"],["zoom"],5,4,13,2.5,16,1.4,19,0.7]
      },
      line:{
        "line-color":"#ff8a00",
        "line-width":["interpolate",["linear"],["zoom"],5,12,10,11,13,9,16,7.5,19,6],
        "line-opacity":1
      },
      highlight:{
        "line-color":"#fff1c7",
        "line-width":["interpolate",["linear"],["zoom"],5,4.4,13,3.7,16,3.1,19,2.6],
        "line-opacity":1
      },
      dash:{
        "line-color":"#ffffff",
        "line-width":["interpolate",["linear"],["zoom"],5,2.6,13,2.3,16,2.0,19,1.7],
        "line-opacity":0.98,
        "line-dasharray":[1.1,1.1]
      }
    };
    const layerPaint={
      [EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_FILL]:paint.fill,
      [EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_OUTER]:paint.outer,
      [EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LINE]:paint.line,
      [EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_HIGHLIGHT]:paint.highlight,
      [EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_DASH]:paint.dash
    };
    Object.entries(layerPaint).forEach(([id,props])=>{
      if(!earthlineMap.getLayer(id))return;
      Object.entries(props).forEach(([k,v])=>earthlineMap.setPaintProperty(id,k,v));
    });
    if(earthlineMap.getLayer(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL)){
      earthlineMap.setLayoutProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-allow-overlap",true);
      earthlineMap.setLayoutProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-ignore-placement",true);
      earthlineMap.setLayoutProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-padding",4);
      earthlineMap.setPaintProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-color","#fff7e6");
      earthlineMap.setPaintProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-halo-color","rgba(35,10,0,1)");
      earthlineMap.setPaintProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-halo-width",5);
      earthlineMap.setPaintProperty(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL,"text-halo-blur",0.2);
    }

    // Keep official aquifers above satellite/road labels and below swale recommendations.
    // The canvas enhancer is still drawn above all Mapbox layers for guaranteed visibility.
    const before=earthlineMap.getLayer(EARTHLINE_SWALE_CASING)?EARTHLINE_SWALE_CASING:undefined;
    [EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_FILL,EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_OUTER,EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LINE,
      EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_HIGHLIGHT,EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_DASH,EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_WELLS_16398,
      EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_EVIDENCE_LABEL_16398,EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_LABEL].forEach(id=>{
      if(earthlineMap.getLayer(id))earthlineMap.moveLayer(id,before);
    });
    return true;
  }catch(e){console.warn("[Earthline] aquifer restyle/order failed",e);return false;}
}
function earthlineEnsureAquiferLayers(){
  if(!earthlineMapActive()||!earthlineMap.getStyle())return false;
  try{
    if(!earthlineMap.getSource(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_SOURCE))earthlineMap.addSource(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_SOURCE,{type:"geojson",data:{type:"FeatureCollection",features:[]}});

    if(!earthlineMap.getLayer(EARTHLINE_AQUIFER_DASH))earthlineMap.addL

      const smoothed=earthlineNaturalContourDisplay(seg);

        const result=earthlineEvaluateOpportunityPath15777(
          safe,targetH,cellM,candidate

  function tileOpportunityMetrics15772(tile,cellM,index){
    let sampled=0,ideal=0,workable=0,noBuild=0,canopy=0,runoffSum=0;
    const maxAcc=Math.max(2,Number(M.maxAcc||2));
    const channelThreshold=Math.max(3,streamThreshold()*2.45);
    const candidates=[];

    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y+=2)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x+=2){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i])continue;
      sampled++;

      const sp=slopePct(x,y);
      if(sp>=3.0&&sp<=4.2)ideal++;
      if(sp>=2.6&&sp<=5.0)workable++;
      if(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i])noBuild++;
      if(M.canopyMask&&M.canopyMask[i])canopy++;

      if(!earthlineOpportunityPointClear15777(x,y))continue;
      if(sp<2.7||sp>4.8)continue;

      const runoff=Math.log1p(M.acc[i]||1)/Math.log1p(maxAcc);
      if(runoff<.07)continue;

      const slopeFit=sp>=3.0&&sp<=4.2
        ?1
        :Math.max(0,1-Math.abs(sp-3.6)/1.25);
      const open=(M.canopyMask&&M.canopyMask[i])?.34:1;
      const channelSetback=Math.max(0,1-(M.acc[i]||0)/channelThreshold);
      const baseScore=Math.round(100*(
        .48*slopeFit+
        .30*runoff+
        .10*open+
        .12*channelSetback
      ));
      runoffSum+=runoff;

      if(baseScore>=42)candidates.push({
        gx:x,gy:y,levelH:hAt(x,y),slopePct:sp,
        baseScore,runoff:Number(runoff.toFixed(3)),open
      });
    }

    candidates.sort((a,b)=>b.baseScore-a.baseScore);
    const hotspots=[];
    for(const c of candidates){
      if(hotspots.length>=8)break;
      if(hotspots.some(h=>Math.hypot(h.gx-c.gx,h.gy-c.gy)<16))continue;

      const corridorResult=opportunityContourPath15777(tile,cellM,c);
      if(!corridorResult)continue;

      const finalScore=Math.round(
        .68*c.baseScore+
        .32*corridorResult.metrics.quality
      );
      const coord=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,c.gx,c.gy);
      const confidence=(
        finalScore>=80 &&
        corridorResult.metrics.quality>=76 &&
        corridorResult.metrics.maxElevError<=1.6
      )?"High":(
        finalScore>=65 &&
        corridorResult.metrics.quality>=62
      )?"Moderate":"Preliminary";

      hotspots.push({
        tile:index,lng:coord[0],lat:coord[1],
        gx:c.gx,gy:c.gy,levelH:c.levelH,
        score:finalScore,
        slopePct:Number(c.slopePct.toFixed(1)),
        runoff:c.runoff,open:c.open,
        corridor:corridorResult.coords,
        pathMetrics:corridorResult.metrics,
        confidence
      });
    }

    const pct=n=>sampled?100*n/sampled:0;
    const rechargePct=Number(
      (M.rechargeStats&&(
        M.rechargeStats.highVeryHighPct!=null
          ?M.rechargeStats.highVeryHighPct
          :M.rechargeStats.modeledPotentialPct
      )) || ((M.donut||[]).slice(0,2).reduce((a,d)=>a+Number(d.p||0),0)) || 0
    );
    const idealPct=pct(ideal),workablePct=pct(workable),noBuildPct=pct(noBuild),canopyPct=pct(canopy);
    const runoffIndex=sampled?runoffSum/sampled:0;
    const tileScore=hotspots.length
      ?Math.round(hotspots.slice(0,4).reduce((a,h)=>a+h.score,0)/Math.min(4,hotspots.length))
      :0;

    return {
      tile:index,lat:tile.lat,lng:tile.lng,
      score:tileScore,
      idealSlopePct:Number(idealPct.toFixed(1)),
      workableSlopePct:Number(workablePct.toFixed(1)),
      noBuildPct:Number(noBuildPct.toFixed(1)),
      canopyPct:Number(canopyPct.toFixed(1)),
      runoffIndex:Number(runoffIndex.toFixed(4)),
      rechargePct:Number(rechargePct.toFixed(1)),
      sampledCells:sampled,
      hotspots
    };
  }

  function rebuildRegionalGrades15772(){
    const completed=(state.tileStats||[]).filter(t=>t&&t.status==="complete"&&t.opportunity);
    const all=[];
    for(const t of completed){
      for(const h of (t.opportunity.hotspots||[])){
        if(h.corridor&&h.corridor.length>=4)all.push({...h});
      }
    }
    all.sort((a,b)=>
      (b.score-a.score) ||
      ((b.pathMetrics&&b.pathMetrics.quality||0)-(a.pathMetrics&&a.pathMetrics.quality||0))
    );

    const ranked=[];
    for(const h of all){
      if(ranked.length>=32)break;
      const near=ranked.some(r=>{
        const dx=(h.lng-r.lng)*metersPerLng((h.lat+r.lat)/2);
        const dy=(h.lat-r.lat)*111320;
        return Math.hypot(dx,dy)<240;
      });
      if(!near)ranked.push(h);
    }

    const gradeOf=score=>score>=80?"A":(score>=66?"B":(score>=53?"C":"D"));
    ranked.forEach((h,i)=>{h.rank=i+1;h.grade=gradeOf(h.score);});

    state.grades={
      type:"FeatureCollection",
      features:ranked.map(h=>({
        type:"Feature",
        properties:{
          grade:h.grade,rank:h.rank,score:h.score,
          slopePct:h.slopePct,runoff:h.runoff,
          confidence:h.confidence,
          pathQuality:h.pathMetrics&&h.pathMetrics.quality||0,
          title:h.grade+h.rank+" · "+h.score+"/100 · "+h.confidence
        },
        geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:[h.lng,h.lat]}
      }))
    };

    state.opportunityLines={
      type:"FeatureCollection",
      features:ranked
        .filter(h=>(h.grade==="A"||h.grade==="B")&&h.corridor&&h.corridor.length>=4)
        .slice(0,12)
        .map(h=>({
          type:"Feature",
          properties:{
            grade:h.grade,rank:h.rank,score:h.score,
            label:h.grade+h.rank+" · "+h.score,
            confidence:h.confidence,
            slopePct:h.slopePct,
            runoff:h.runoff,
            pathQuality:h.pathMetrics&&h.pathMetrics.quality||0,
            meanSlope:h.pathMetrics&&h.pathMetrics.meanSlope||0,
            maxSlope:h.pathMetrics&&h.pathMetrics.maxSlope||0,
            contourErrorM:h.pathMetrics&&h.pathMetrics.meanElevError||0,
            lengthM:h.pathMetrics&&h.pathMetrics.lengthM||0,
            constructionReady:false,
            scale:"regional-opportunity"
          },
          geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:h.corridor}
        }))
    };
    const opportunityGradeCounts16153={A:0,B:0,C:0};
    for(const feature of state.opportunityLines.features){
      const p=feature.properties||(feature.properties={});
      const g=/^[ABC]$/.test(String(p.grade||''))?String(p.grade):'C';
      const r=++opportunityGradeCounts16153[g];
      p.rank=r;p.display_rank=r;p.display_code=g+r;p.label=g+r;
    }

    state.rankedOpportunities=ranked;
  }

  function contourNiceStep15775(raw){
    const power=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(Math.max(.001,raw))));
    const n=raw/power;
    const nice=n<=1?1:(n<=2?2:(n<=5?5:10));
    return nice*power;
  }

  function contourDataForTile15772(tile,cellM,tileIndex){
    const features=[];
    let minH=Infinity,maxH=-Infinity;
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
      if(!M.inP[i])continue;
      const h=Number(M.h[i]);
      if(!Number.isFinite(h))continue;
      minH=Math.min(minH,h);maxH=Math.max(maxH,h);
    }
    if(!Number.isFinite(minH)||!Number.isFinite(maxH)||maxH-minH<1)return features;

    const range=maxH-minH;
    const step=contourNiceStep15775(range/6);
    const first=Math.ceil(minH/step)*step;
    let levelIndex=0;

    for(let L=first;L<maxH&&levelIndex<7;L+=step,levelIndex++){
      const lines=[];
      let labelCoord=null;

      const cross=(a,b)=>(
        (a<=L&&b>L)||(a>L&&b<=L)
      );
      const interp=(a,b)=>{
        const d=b-a;
        return Math.abs(d)<1e-9?.5:(L-a)/d;
      };

      for(let y=0;y<GH-1;y++)for(let x=0;x<GW-1;x++){
        const h00=Number(M.h[idx(x,y)]);
        const h10=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y)]);
        const h01=Number(M.h[idx(x,y+1)]);
        const h11=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y+1)]);
        if(!Number.isFinite(h00)||!Number.isFinite(h10)||!Number.isFinite(h01)||!Number.isFinite(h11))continue;

        const pts=[];
        if(cross(h00,h10))pts.push([x+interp(h00,h10),y]);
        if(cross(h10,h11))pts.push([x+1,y+interp(h10,h11)]);
        if(cross(h01,h11))pts.push([x+interp(h01,h11),y+1]);
        if(cross(h00,h01))pts.push([x,y+interp(h00,h01)]);

        if(pts.length===2){
          const a=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);
          const b=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,pts[1][0],pts[1][1]);
          lines.push([a,b]);
        }else if(pts.length===4){
          const a=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);
          const b=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,pts[1][0],pts[1][1]);
          const c=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,pts[2][0],pts[2][1]);
          const d=gridToLngLat(tile,cellM,pts[3][0],pts[3][1]);
          lines.push([a,b],[c,d]);
        }

        if(lines.length>=360)break;
      }

      if(!lines.length)continue;
      const major=levelIndex%2===0;
      features.push({
        type:"Feature",
        properties:{tile:tileIndex,major,elevM:Math.round(Number((M.loc&&M.loc.elevM)||0)+L)},
        geometry:{type:"MultiLineString",coordinates:lines}
      });

      if(major){
        const seg=lines[Math.floor(lines.length/2)];
        labelCoord=[(seg[0][0]+seg[1][0])/2,(seg[0][1]+seg[1][1])/2];
        const absM=Math.round(Number((M.loc&&M.loc.elevM)||0)+L);
        features.push({
          type:"Feature",
          properties:{tile:tileIndex,major:true,elevM:absM,label:absM.toLocaleString()+" m"},
          geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:labelCoord}
        });
      }
    }
    return features;
  }

  function rawSpacingSamples(raw){
    const nearest=[];
    for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){
      let best=Infinity;
      const a=raw[i].coords||[],sa=Math.max(1,Math.floor(a.length/24));
      for(let j=0;j<raw.length;j++){
        if(i===j||raw[i].bandKey===raw[j].bandKey)continue;
        const b=raw[j].coords||[],sb=Math.max(1,Math.floor(b.length/24));
        for(let ai=0;ai<a.length;ai+=sa)for(let bi=0;bi<b.length;bi+=sb)best=Math.min(best,haversineM(a[ai],b[bi]));
      }
      if(Number.isFinite(best))nearest.push(best);
    }
    return nearest;
  }
  function tileSite(tile,parent,index){
    return {
      name:"Regional Opportunity Tile "+index,sub:"Real DEM corridor tile",fullName:"Regional Opportunity Tile "+index,
      countryCode:parent.countryCode||earthlineCountryCode(parent)||"",lat:tile.lat,lng:tile.lng,zoomHint:16.6,placeType:"selected_site",
      userSelectedSite:true,planning:false,acres:tile.acres,regionalMode:false,propertyMode:true,
      elevM:Number(parent.elevM||0),trendDeg:Number(parent.trendDeg||180),trendSlope:Number(parent.trendSlope||6),
      relief:Number(parent.relief||8),rough:Number(parent.rough||1),runC:Number(parent.runC||.35),precipIn:Number(parent.precipIn||30),
      zones:["Regional Tile"],analysisBoundaryLngLat:tile.poly.slice(0,-1)
    };
  }
  function ensureArrays(){
    for(const k of ["h","fill","acc"]){if(!M[k]||M[k].length!==N)M[k]=new Float32Array(N);else M[k].fill(k==="acc"?1:0);}
    if(!M.dir||M.dir.length!==N)M.dir=new Int8Array(N);M.dir.fill(-1);
    for(const k of ["inP","pond","roadMask","buildingMask","noBuildMask","hardCoreMask","vectorNoBuildMask","shadeMask","canopyMask","erosionMask","soilMask"]){
      if(!M[k]||M[k].length!==N)M[k]=new Uint8Array(N);else M[k].fill(0);
    }
  }
  function analysisCellM(site){
    const areaM2=Math.max(4046.8564224*2,Number(site.acres||20)*4046.8564224);
    const w=Math.sqrt(areaM2*1.15),h=areaM2/w;
    return clampN(Math.max(w/(GW*.82),h/(GH*.82)),1,7.45);
  }
  function clearTile(site){
    ensureArrays();
    M.loc=site;M.zone="Regional Tile";M.centerLng=site.lng;M.centerLat=site.lat;M.viewZoom=16.6;
    M.analysisBoundaryLngLat=site.analysisBoundaryLngLat.map(p=>p.slice());M.analysisBoundary=[];
    M.requestedCellM=analysisCellM(site);M.targetSwaleSpacingM=TARGET_M;M.swCount=360;
    M.terrainReady=false;M.previewReady=false;M.demReady=false;M.analysisReady=false;M.terrainStamp="";M.previewStamp="";
    M.swales=[];M.rechZones=[];M.donut=[];M.donutCenter=null;M.rechargeStats=null;M.spacingStats=null;M.vectorNoBuildCoverage=null;M.maxAcc=1;M.outFlow=0;M.pondCells=0;
    M.satCanvas=null;M.realMode=false;M._noBuildStamp="";M.vectorNoBuildStamp="";M.lastTiming={};
    try{if(typeof _soilData!=="undefined")_soilData=null;}catch(e){}
  }

  const CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS_15778={elevation:.20,rainfall:.15,soils:.15,landCover:.15,roadsStructures:.15,hydrography:.10,hydrogeology:.10};
  const clampScore15778=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(Number(v)||0)));
  function qualityElevation15778(ctx){
    const complete=ctx.analysisCells>0&&ctx.finiteElevationCells/ctx.analysisCells>=.98;
    const score=(M.demReady&&complete)?92:(complete?72:35);
    return {score,basis:(M.demReady?"Real DEM loaded":"Terrain array available without confirmed DEM flag")+"; "+ctx.finiteElevationCells+"/"+ctx.analysisCells+" analyzed cells contain finite elevation values."};
  }
  function qualityRainfall15778(ctx){
    const ok=Number.isFinite(ctx.precipIn)&&ctx.precipIn>0;
    return {score:ok?48:0,basis:ok?("Annual precipitation value "+ctx.precipIn+" in is available, but the current engine has no dated intensity/seasonality metadata."):"No usable precipitation value."};
  }
  function qualitySoils15778(){
    const present=typeof _soilData!=="undefined"&&!!_soilData;
    return {score:present?78:25,basis:present?"Soil/percolation data loaded for this analysis.":"No analysis-ready soil layer loaded; terrain and land-cover proxies only."};
  }
  function qualityLandCover15778(){
    const classified=!!(M.satCanvas&&typeof _canopyData!=="undefined"&&_canopyData&&typeof _impervData!=="undefined"&&_impervData);
    return {score:classified?74:30,basis:classified?"Satellite image classified on the hydrology grid for canopy and impervious cover.":"No completed satellite canopy/impervious classification."};
  }
  function qualityRoadsStructures15778(){
    const v=M.vectorNoBuildCoverage||{};
    const verified=!!v.verified;
    return {score:verified?(v.layerCount>0?88:72):0,basis:verified?("Mapped road/building exclusion query verified; "+Number(v.layerCount||0)+" source layers and "+Number(v.featureCount||0)+" processed features."):"Road/building exclusion coverage was not verified. Terrain-derived hydrology remains valid; corridor placement requires imagery and field verification."};
  }
  function qualityHydrography15778(){
    const routed=!!(M.dir&&M.acc&&Number(M.maxAcc||0)>1);
    return {score:routed?82:20,basis:routed?("D8 flow routing completed; maximum accumulation "+Math.round(Number(M.maxAcc||0))+" cells."):"Flow-routing arrays are incomplete."};
  }
  function qualityHydrogeology15778(ctx){
    const us=ctx.countryCode==="us";
    const official=us&&Array.isArray(M.usgsAquifers)&&M.usgsAquifers.length>0;
    return {score:official?86:(us?45:20),basis:official?("Official US aquifer context loaded; "+M.usgsAquifers.length+" mapped feature(s)."):us?"US location, but no intersecting official aquifer feature is active.":"No verified national aquifer dataset is connected for this country; recharge is modeled from terrain, runoff, land cover, and available soils."};
  }
  function confidenceV0FromCurrent15778(ctx){
    const components={
      elevation:qualityElevation15778(ctx),rainfall:qualityRainfall15778(ctx),soils:qualitySoils15778(),
      landCover:qualityLandCover15778(),roadsStructures:qualityRoadsStructures15778(),
      hydrography:qualityHydrography15778(),hydrogeology:qualityHydrogeology15778(ctx)
    };
    let score=0;
    for(const [key,weight] of Object.entries(CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS_15778))score+=weight*clampScore15778(components[key].score);
    return {score:Number(score.toFixed(1)),version:"Confidence v0",components,fieldVerification:"Not verified"};
  }
  function normalizeDistribution15778(values){
    const raw=(values||[]).slice(0,5).map(v=>Math.max(0,Number(v)||0));
    while(raw.length<5)raw.push(0);
    let total=raw.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    if(total<=0){raw[2]=100;total=100;}
    const p=raw.map(v=>Number((v/total*100).toFixed(1)));
    const diff=Number((100-p.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)).toFixed(1));
    p[2]=Number((p[2]+diff).toFixed(1));
    return p;
  }
  function donutObjects15778(p){
    const names=["Very High","High","Moderate","Low","Very Low"],colors=["#e53935","#fb8c00","#fdd835","#43a047","#1e88e5"];
    return normalizeDistribution15778(p).map((v,i)=>({n:names[i],c:colors[i],p:v}));
  }
  function snapshotTile15778(site,opportunity){
    let sum=0,analysisCells=0,finiteElevationCells=0,noBuild=0;
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++)if(M.inP[i]){
      analysisCells++;if(Number.isFinite(Number(M.h[i]))){sum+=Number(M.h[i]);finiteElevationCells++;}
      if(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i])noBuild++;
    }
    const countryCode=String(site.countryCode||earthlineCountryCode(site)||"").toLowerCase().slice(0,2);
    const ctx={analysisCells,finiteElevationCells,precipIn:Number(site.precipIn||0),countryCode};
    const current=(M.donut||[]).map(x=>Number(x&&x.p||0));
    const dist=normalizeDistribution15778(current.length?current:[0,0,100,0,0]);
    const meanElevationM=Number(site.elevM||0)+(finiteElevationCells?sum/finiteElevationCells:0);
    return {
      analysisCells,finiteElevationCells,meanElevationM,meanElevationFt:meanElevationM*3.28084,
      rechargeDistribution:dist,rechargeScore:Number((dist[0]+dist[1]).toFixed(1)),
      rechargeBasis:(M.rechargeStats&&M.rechargeStats.analyzedCells)?"Modeled cell distribution":"Modeled distribution; detailed source metadata unavailable",
      runoffCoefficient:Number(site.runC||.35),precipIn:Number(site.precipIn||30),
      noBuildPct:analysisCells?Number((noBuild/analysisCells*100).toFixed(1)):0,
      exclusions:Object.assign({},M.vectorNoBuildCoverage||{}),
      confidence:confidenceV0FromCurrent15778(ctx),countryCode,opportunity
    };
  }
  function aggregateRegional15778(){
    const tiles=(state.tileStats||[]).filter(t=>t&&t.status==="complete"&&t.snapshot);
    const weights=tiles.map(t=>Math.max(1,Number(t.snapshot.analysisCells||1)));
    const totalWeight=weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)||1;
    const dist=[0,0,0,0,0];
    let elev=0,runC=0,precip=0,noBuild=0,confidence=0;
    for(let k=0;k<tiles.length;k++){
      const s=tiles[k].snapshot,w=weights[k];
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++)dist[i]+=Number(s.rechargeDistribution[i]||0)*w;
      elev+=Number(s.meanElevationM||0)*w;runC+=Number(s.runoffCoefficient||0)*w;
      precip+=Number(s.precipIn||0)*w;noBuild+=Number(s.noBuildPct||0)*w;
      confidence+=Number(s.confidence&&s.confidence.score||0)*w;
    }
    const normalized=normalizeDistribution15778(dist.map(v=>v/totalWeight));
    const countryCode=String((state.parent&&state.parent.countryCode)||earthlineCountryCode(state.parent||{})||"").toLowerCase().slice(0,2);
    const confidenceComponents={};
    for(const key of Object.keys(CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS_15778)){
      let weighted=0,bases=[];
      for(let k=0;k<tiles.length;k++){
        const c=tiles[k].snapshot.confidence.components[key],w=weights[k];
        weighted+=Number(c&&c.score||0)*w;if(c&&c.basis&&!bases.includes(c.basis))bases.push(c.basis);
      }
      confidenceComponents[key]={score:Number((weighted/totalWeight).toFixed(1)),basis:bases.slice(0,3).join(" | ")};
    }
    const corridorFeatures=(state.opportunityLines&&state.opportunityLines.features)||[];
    const corridorLengthM=corridorFeatures.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+lineLengthM(f.geometry&&f.geometry.coordinates||[]),0);
    const ranked=state.rankedOpportunities||[];
    const gradeA=ranked.filter(x=>x.grade==="A").length,gradeB=ranked.filter(x=>x.grade==="B").length;
    const totGph=tiles.reduce((a,t)=>a+Number(t.totGph||0),0),outGph=tiles.reduce((a,t)=>a+Number(t.outGph||0),0);
    return {
      mode:"regional",completedTiles:tiles.length,plannedTiles:(state.tiles||[]).length,
      rechargeDistribution:normalized,rechargeScore:Number((normalized[0]+normalized[1]).toFixed(1)),
      meanElevationM:elev/totalWeight,meanElevationFt:elev/totalWeight*3.28084,
      runoffCoefficient:runC/totalWeight,precipIn:precip/totalWeight,
      tributaries:tiles.reduce((a,t)=>a+Number(t.tribs||0),0),outGph,
      noBuildPct:noBuild/totalWeight,corridorCount:corridorFeatures.length,corridorLengthM,
      gradeA,gradeB,topOpportunity:ranked[0]||null,runoffInterceptedGph:totGph,
      runoffInterceptPct:Math.min(95,Math.round(totGph/Math.max(1,outGph)*100)),
      countryCode,fieldVerification:"Not verified",
      confidence:{score:Number((confidence/totalWeight).toFixed(1)),version:"Confidence v0",components:confidenceComponents,fieldVerification:"Not verified"}
    };
  }
  function confidenceTitle15778(conf){
    if(!conf||!conf.components)return "Confidence basis unavailable";
    return Object.entries(conf.components).map(([k,v])=>k+" "+Number(v.score||0).toFixed(0)+"/100 — "+v.basis).join("\n");
  }

  function applyRechargePanel15778(summary){
    const outsideUS=summary.countryCode!=="us";
    const verifiedAquifer=!!(
      !outsideUS &&
      M.usgsAquifers &&
      M.usgsAquifers.length
    );
    setSidebarText15778(
      "aquiferPanelTitle",
      outsideUS?"MODELED RECHARGE OPPORTUNITY":"AQUIFER RECHARGE POTENTIAL"
    );
    setSidebarText15778("aquiferStatus",outsideUS
      ?(
        "Modeled recharge opportunity: "+Number(summary.rechargeScore||0).toFixed(1)+
        "%. This score estimates where rainfall may be slowed and infiltrated; it does not prove a connection to a mapped aquifer. Field and hydrogeological verification required."
      )
      :"Official aquifer context where available; infiltration and recharge values remain modeled and require field verification."
    );
    const truth=q("earthlineAquiferTruth15782");
    if(truth){
      truth.className=verifiedAquifer?"verified":"modeled";
      truth.textContent=verifiedAquifer
        ?"Verified aquifer context active — recharge magnitude remains modeled."
        :"Modeled opportunity only — no verified aquifer boundary active.";
    }
    M.donut=donutObjects15778(summary.rechargeDistribution);
    M.donutCenter=Number(summary.rechargeScore||0);
    M.rechargeStats={
      analyzedTiles:summary.completedTiles||1,
      modeledPotentialPct:M.donutCenter,
      highVeryHighPct:(summary.mode==="property"&&Number.isFinite(Number(summary.highVeryHighPct)))
        ?Number(summary.highVeryHighPct)
        :M.donutCenter,
      verifiedAquiferContext:verifiedAquifer
    };
    try{drawDonut();earthlineUpdateRechargeSvg();}
    catch(e){console.warn("[Earthline] recharge sidebar render",e);}
  }
  function renderSidebar15778(mode,summary,partial=false){
    if(typeof window.earthlineEnforceUniqueUI15784==="function"){
      window.earthlineEnforceUniqueUI15784();
    }

  function corridorStats(){
    const sw=M.swales||[];let lenFt=0,totGph=0,topGph=0;
    for(const s of sw){
      lenFt+=Number(s.lenM||0)*3.28084;
      const cap=typeof swaleGph==="function"?swaleGph(s):0;
      totGph+=cap;topGph=Math.max(topGph,cap);
    }
    return {lenFt,totGph,topGph};
  }
    function updatePanels15778(partial){
    state.summary=aggregateRegional15778();

  }
    async function analyzeTile(tile,parent,index,gen){
    const tileStarted16191=performance.now();
    let lastError=null;
    for(let attempt=1;attempt<=2;attempt++){
      try{
        clearTile(tileSite(tile,parent,index));
        earthlineProgress("dem","tile "+index+" of "+state.tiles.length+" · attempt "+attempt+"…");
        await waitFrame();
        const ok=await rebuildModel(true,gen);
        if(!ok||earthlineIsStale(gen))throw new Error(earthlineIsStale(gen)?"Analysis cancelled":"Terrain or model unavailable");
        if(!(M.vectorNoBuildCoverage&&M.vectorNoBuildCoverage.verified)){
          M.exclusionConfidence="unverified";
          console.warn("[Earthline] "+BUILD+" tile "+index+" has unverified road/structure coverage; terrain-derived output retained and confidence reduced.");
        }else{
          M.exclusionConfidence="verified";
        }
        const cellM=Number(M.cellM||analysisCellM(M.loc));
        // Freeze the exact tile transform. No later global model mutation can move this output.
        M.centerLat=tile.lat;M.centerLng=tile.lng;
        const opportunity=tileOpportunityMetrics15772(tile,cellM,index);
        const snapshot=snapshotTile15778(M.loc,opportunity);
        const priorStat={opportunity,snapshot};
        state.tileStats[index-1]=priorStat;
        const sw=swaleDataForTile(tile,cellM,index),rz=rechargeDataForTile(tile,cellM,index),fl=flowDataForTile(tile,cellM,index);
        const contourFeatures=contourDataForTile15772(tile,cellM,index);
        const cs=corridorStats(),thr=streamThreshold(),trib=censusTributaries(thr);
        appendUnique(state.swales.features,sw.features);appendUnique(state.recharge.features,rz);appendUnique(state.flow.features,fl);
        appendUnique(state.contours.features,contourFeatures);
        state.rawCorridors.push(...sw.raw);
        // Spacing is measured within each independently resolved DEM tile. This avoids
        // unrelated corridors miles apart from distorting the 15–25 ft prescription metric.
        state.spacingSamples.push(...rawSpacingSamples(sw.raw));
        tile.status="complete";
        return {
          status:"complete",tile:index,lat:tile.lat,lng:tile.lng,
          swales:sw.raw.length,lenFt:cs.lenFt,totGph:cs.totGph,topGph:cs.topGph,
          outGph:gph(M.outFlow),tribs:trib.sources,attempts:attempt,
          opportunity,snapshot,
          confidence:snapshot.confidence,rechargeDistribution:snapshot.rechargeDistribution,
          rechargeScore:snapshot.rechargeScore,meanElevationFt:snapshot.meanElevationFt,
          runoffCoefficient:snapshot.runoffCoefficient,exclusions:snapshot.exclusions,
          timings:Object.assign({},M.lastTiming||{},{tileTotalMs:Math.round(performance.now()-tileStarted16191)})
        };
      }catch(err){
        lastError=err;
        if(earthlineIsStale(gen))throw err;
        console.warn("[Earthline] "+BUILD+" tile "+index+" attempt "+attempt+" failed",err);
        if(attempt<2)await waitMs(300);
      }
    }
    tile.status=/exclusion/i.test(String(lastError&&lastError.message))?"withheld":"failed";
    return {status:tile.status,tile:index,error:String(lastError&&lastError.message||"unknown"),timings:{tileTotalMs:Math.round(performance.now()-tileStarted16191)}};
  }

  


  /* BUILD-CHECK-15821 — authoritative property-frame ownership.
     The map camera controls presentation. The selected property identity controls
     the scientific center, boundary, acreage, cell size, and internal DEM zoom. */
  function earthlinePolygonAreaAcres15821(points){
    if(!Array.isArray(points)||points.length<3)return 0;
    const meanLat=points.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+Number(p&&p[1]||0),0)/points.length;
    const mx=111320*Math.max(0.05,Math.cos(meanLat*Math.PI/180));
    const my=111320;
    let area=0;
    for(let i=0,j=points.length-1;i<points.length;j=i++){
      const xi=Number(points[i][0])*mx;
      const yi=Number(points[i][1])*my;
      const xj=Number(points[j][0])*mx;
      const yj=Number(points[j][1])*my;
      area+=(xj*yi)-(xi*yj);
    }
    return Math.abs(area)*0.5/4046.8564224;
  }

  function earthlineBoundarySignature15821(points){
    return (Array.isArray(points)?points:[]).map(p=>[
      Number(Number(p&&p[0]||0).toFixed(8)),
      Number(Number(p&&p[1]||0).toFixed(8))
    ]);
  }

  function earthlinePropertyIdentity15821(parent,anchor){
    const parentLat=Number(parent&&parent.lat);
    const parentLng=Number(parent&&parent.lng);
    const hasParentPoint=Number.isFinite(parentLat)&&Number.isFinite(parentLng);
    const center={
      lat:hasParentPoint?parentLat:Number(anchor&&anchor.lat),
      lng:hasParentPoint?parentLng:Number(anchor&&anchor.lng)
    };
    const placeType=String(parent&&parent.placeType||"").toLowerCase();
    const search=String(M&&M.appliedSearchText||"").trim().toLowerCase();
    const name=String(
      parent&&(parent.fullName||parent.name||parent.sub)||"selected-property"
    ).trim().toLowerCase();
    return{
      key:[
        search||name,
        placeType||"property",
        Number(center.lat).toFixed(6),
        Number(center.lng).toFixed(6)
      ].join("|"),
      center,
      placeType,
      search,
      name
    };
  }


  /* BUILD-CHECK-15827 — one visible, deliberate scientific-frame owner.
     Manual camera movement never changes the scientific frame. A frame changes
     only after a new search, a drawn boundary, or the explicit current-view
     control installed by this build. */
  function earthlineFrameSearchKey15827(frame){
    if(!frame)return "";
    if(frame.searchKey!=null)return String(frame.searchKey);
    const identity=String(frame.identityKey||"");
    return identity.split("|")[0]||"";
  }

  function earthlineCurrentSearchKey15827(){
    return String(M&&M.appliedSearchText||"")
      .trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g," ");
  }

  function earthlineFrameMatchesSearch15827(frame){
    return !!(
      frame&&
      earthlineFrameSearchKey15827(frame)===
        earthlineCurrentSearchKey15827()
    );
  }

  function earthlineRunFrameDirective15827(context){
    if(context&&context.resolvedSearchLoc)return null;
    const searchKey=earthlineCurrentSearchKey15827();
    const pending=state.pendingAnalysisFrame15827||
      M.pendingAnalysisFrame15827||null;

    if(pending&&String(pending.searchKey||"")===searchKey){
      return Object.assign({
        reason:"explicit-current-view-pending"
      },pending);
    }

    const frame=M.authoritativePropertyFrame15821||
      state.authoritativePropertyFrame15821||null;
    if(frame&&earthlineFrameMatchesSearch15827(frame)){
      return{
        reason:"locked-scientific-frame",
        center:{
          lng:Number(frame.center.lng),
          lat:Number(frame.center.lat)
        },
        boundary:(frame.boundary||[]).map(p=>[
          Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])
        ]),
        acres:Number(frame.acres||20),
        internalAnalysisZoom:Number(
          frame.internalAnalysisZoom||18
        ),
        source:String(frame.source||"locked-frame"),
        searchKey
      };
    }
    return null;
  }
