  function earthlineResolvePropertyFrame15821(parent,anchor){
    const identity=earthlinePropertyIdentity15821(parent,anchor);
    const searchKey=earthlineCurrentSearchKey15827();
    const pending=state.pendingAnalysisFrame15827||
      M.pendingAnalysisFrame15827||null;
    const existing=M.authoritativePropertyFrame15821||
      state.authoritativePropertyFrame15821||null;

    let center={
      lng:Number(identity.center.lng),
      lat:Number(identity.center.lat)
    };
    let acres=Math.max(
      8,
      Math.min(80,Number(parent&&parent.acres||20))
    );
    let boundary=null;
    let source="fixed-property-analysis-extent";

    if(pending&&String(pending.searchKey||"")===searchKey){
      center={
        lng:Number(pending.center.lng),
        lat:Number(pending.center.lat)
      };
      acres=Number(pending.acres);
      boundary=(pending.boundary||[]).map(p=>[
        Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])
      ]);
      source=String(
        pending.source||"explicit-current-view-analysis-area"
      );
      state.pendingAnalysisFrame15827=null;
      M.pendingAnalysisFrame15827=null;
    }else{
      const drawnBoundary=(
        parent&&
        String(parent.placeType||"").toLowerCase()==="drawn_site"&&
        Array.isArray(parent.analysisBoundaryLngLat)&&
        parent.analysisBoundaryLngLat.length>=3
      )
        ?parent.analysisBoundaryLngLat.map(p=>[
          Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])
        ])
        :null;

      if(drawnBoundary){
        boundary=drawnBoundary;
        const measured=earthlinePolygonAreaAcres15821(boundary);
        if(Number.isFinite(measured)&&measured>=2){
          acres=Math.max(8,Math.min(80,measured));
        }
        source="explicit-drawn-analysis-boundary";
      }else if(existing&&earthlineFrameMatchesSearch15827(existing)){
        return existing;
      }else{
        const explicitBoundary=(
          parent&&parent.userSelectedSite&&
          Array.isArray(parent.analysisBoundaryLngLat)&&
          parent.analysisBoundaryLngLat.length>=3
        )
          ?parent.analysisBoundaryLngLat.map(p=>[
            Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])
          ])
          :null;

        if(explicitBoundary){
          boundary=explicitBoundary;
          const measured=earthlinePolygonAreaAcres15821(boundary);
          if(Number.isFinite(measured)&&measured>=2){
            acres=Math.max(8,Math.min(80,measured));
          }
          source="explicit-selected-analysis-boundary";
        }else{
          boundary=earthlineRectangleAround(
            center.lng,
            center.lat,
            acres
          );
        }
      }
    }

    if(!Array.isArray(boundary)||boundary.length<3){
      boundary=earthlineRectangleAround(
        center.lng,
        center.lat,
        acres
      );
      source="fixed-property-analysis-extent";
    }

    const internalAnalysisZoom=18;
    const frame={
      build:"BUILD-CHECK-15827",
      owner:"earthlineResolvePropertyFrame15821",
      searchKey,
      identityKey:[
        searchKey||identity.name,
        String(parent&&parent.placeType||identity.placeType||"property"),
        Number(center.lat).toFixed(6),
        Number(center.lng).toFixed(6)
      ].join("|"),
      center:{
        lng:Number(center.lng),
        lat:Number(center.lat)
      },
      acres:Number(acres),
      internalAnalysisZoom,
      boundary:earthlineBoundarySignature15821(boundary),
      source,
      viewportIndependent:true,
      changesOnlyByExplicitAction:true,
      resolvedAt:Date.now()
    };

    state.authoritativePropertyFrame15821=frame;
    M.authoritativePropertyFrame15821=frame;

    if(
      source!=="explicit-current-view-analysis-area"&&
      source!=="restored-selected-property-frame"
    ){
      state.selectedPropertyBaseFrame15827=
        JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(frame));
    }

    return frame;
  }

  function earthlineRawPropertyGeoJSON15816(){
    const features=[];
    const raw=Array.isArray(M&&M.swales)?M.swales:[];
    for(let rawIndex=0;rawIndex<raw.length;rawIndex++){
      const corridor=raw[rawIndex];
      const points=corridor&&Array.isArray(corridor.pts)?corridor.pts:[];
      const coordinates=[];
      for(const point of points){
        if(!Array.isArray(point)||point.length<2)continue;
        const ll=earthlineGridToLngLat(Number(point[0]),Number(point[1]));
        if(
          Array.isArray(ll)&&ll.length>=2&&
          Number.isFinite(Number(ll[0]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(ll[1]))
        ){
          coordinates.push([Number(ll[0]),Number(ll[1])]);
        }
      }
      if(coordinates.length<2)continue;
      features.push({
        type:"Feature",
        properties:{
          id:String(corridor.id||rawIndex+1),
          rawIndex,
          score:Number(corridor.score||0),
          isSite:!!corridor.isSite,
          scale:"property",
          source:"prescribeSwales15778",
          publicationOwner:"earthlineRawPropertyGeoJSON15816"
        },
        geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates}
      });
    }
    return {type:"FeatureCollection",features};
  }

  function degenerateCamera15801(anchor,zoom){
    const lat=Number(anchor&&anchor.lat),lng=Number(anchor&&anchor.lng),z=Number(zoom);
    if(!Number.isFinite(lat)||!Number.isFinite(lng)||!Number.isFinite(z))return true;
    // Exact integer-pair centers are common startup/fallback signatures, not
    // defensible property targets. This is intentionally narrow to avoid
    // rejecting legitimate coordinates merely near a round number.
    const integerPair=
      Math.abs(lat-Math.round(lat))<1e-7&&
      Math.abs(lng-Math.round(lng))<1e-7;
    return integerPair;
  }

  function integrityToleranceM15801(identity,mode){
    const pt=String(identity&&identity.placeType||"").toLowerCase();
    if(mode==="property"||pt==="address"||pt==="selected_site"||pt==="drawn_site")return 250;
    if(pt==="poi"||pt==="neighborhood")return 1000;
    if(pt==="city"||pt==="place"||pt==="locality")return 5000;
    return 10000;
  }

  function earthlineFinalizeCandidateAccounting15840(diag){

      zone15840.flowFirstCandidates=selection15840.filter(r=>r.zone===zone15840.id&&r.candidateOrigin15840==="flow-first").length;

  function earthlineFinalizeSeedFates15840(diag){

  function earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx){
    let lo=0,hi=levels.length-1;
    while(lo<=hi){
      const mid=(lo+hi)>>1,v=levels[mid];
      if(v<mn)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid-1;
    }
    return lo<levels.length&&levels[lo]<=mx;
  }
  function earthlineMeasureContourCells15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr){
    if(!diag||!levels||!levels.length)return;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS)continue;
      const h00=Number(M.h[idx(x,y)]),h10=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y)]);
      const h01=Number(M.h[idx(x,y+1)]),h11=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y+1)]);
      if(![h00,h10,h01,h11].every(Number.isFinite))continue;
      const mn=Math.min(h00,h10,h01,h11),mx=Math.max(h00,h10,h01,h11);
      if(mx-mn>1e-7&&earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx)){
        earthlineCoverageZone15803(diag,x,y).contourCells++;
        diag.totals.contourCells++;
      }
    }
  }
  function earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales){
    const mask=new Uint8Array(Math.max(1,N));
    const mark=(x,y)=>{
      const ix=Math.round(x),iy=Math.round(y);
      if(ix>=0&&iy>=0&&ix<GW&&iy<GH)mask[idx(ix,iy)]=1;
    };
    for(const sw of (swales||[])){
      const pts=sw&&sw.pts||[];
      for(let j=0;j<pts.length;j++){
        mark(pts[j][0],pts[j][1]);
        if(j===0)continue;
        const a=pts[j-1],b=pts[j];
        const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0]),Math.abs(b[1]-a[1]))*2));
        for(let k=1;k<steps;k++){
          const t=k/steps;mark(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t);
        }
      }
    }
    return mask;
  }
  function earthlineEDT1D15803(f,n){
    const v=new Int32Array(n),z=new Float64Array(n+1),d=new Float64Array(n);
    let k=0;v[0]=0;z[0]=-Infinity;z[1]=Infinity;
    for(let q=1;q<n;q++){
      let s;
      do{
        const p=v[k];
        s=((f[q]+q*q)-(f[p]+p*p))/(2*q-2*p);
        if(s<=z[k])k--;
      }while(s<=z[k]&&k>=0);
      k++;v[k]=q;z[k]=s;z[k+1]=Infinity;
    }
    k=0;
    for(let q=0;q<n;q++){
      while(z[k+1]<q)k++;
      const p=v[k];d[q]=(q-p)*(q-p)+f[p];
    }
    return d;
  }
  function earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask){
    const BIG=1e12,tmp=new Float64Array(N),out=new Float64Array(N);
    for(let y=0;y<GH;y++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)f[x]=corridorMask[idx(x,y)]?0:BIG;
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)tmp[idx(x,y)]=d[x];
    }
    for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)f[y]=tmp[idx(x,y)];
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)out[idx(x,y)]=Math.sqrt(d[y])*Math.max(.001,Number(M.cellM||1));
    }
    return out;
  }
  function earthlineBuildBoundaryEvidence15803(diag){
    const features=[];
    let poly=(M.analysisBoundaryLngLat||[]).map(p=>[Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])]).filter(p=>p.every(Number.isFinite));
    const frame15875=M.authoritativePropertyFrame15821||null;
    let acres15875=Number(frame15875&&frame15875.acres);
    if(!Number.isFinite(acres15875)||acres15875<=0)acres15875=Number(M.loc&&M.loc.acres);

      acres15875=earthlinePolygonAreaM2(poly)/4046.8564224;
    }
    const roundedAcres15875=Number.isFinite(acres15875)&&acres15875>0
      ?(Math.abs(acres15875-Math.round(acres15875))<0.05?String(Math.round(acres15875)):acres15875.toFixed(1))
      :"";
    const boundaryLabel15875=roundedAcres15875?roundedAcres15875+"-acre boundary":"Analysis boundary";
    if(poly.length>=3){
      if(poly[0][0]!==poly[poly.length-1][0]||poly[0][1]!==poly[poly.length-1][1])poly.push(poly[0].slice());
      features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"boundary",label:"Exact analysis boundary",boundaryLabel:boundaryLabel15875},geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:poly}});
      diag.boundary={type:"analysisBoundaryLngLat",coordinates:poly,label:boundaryLabel15875};
    }else{
      const ring=[[0,0],[GW,0],[GW,GH],[0,GH],[0,0]].map(p=>earthlineGridToLngLat(p[0],p[1]));
      features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"boundary",label:"Analysis-grid extent (boundary unavailable)",boundaryLabel:boundaryLabel15875},geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:ring}});
      diag.boundary={type:"gridExtentFallback",coordinates:ring,label:boundaryLabel15875};
    }
    const addClipped=(vertical,pos,index)=>{
      let active=null;
      const limit=vertical?GH:GW;
      for(let a=0;a<=limit;a++){
        const x=vertical?Math.max(0,Math.min(GW-1,Math.round(pos))):Math.max(0,Math.min(GW-1,a));
        const y=vertical?Math.max(0,Math.min(GH-1,a)):Math.max(0,Math.min(GH-1,Math.round(pos)));
        const inside=a<limit&&M.inP&&M.inP[idx(x,y)];
        if(inside&&!active)active=[];
        if(inside)active.push(earthlineGridToLngLat(vertical?pos:a,vertical?a:pos));
        if((!inside||a===limit)&&active){
          if(active.length>=2)features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"grid",index},geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:active}});
          active=null;
        }
      }
    };
    for(let i=1;i<4;i++){addClipped(true,GW*i/4,"V"+i);addClipped(false,GH*i/4,"H"+i);}
    for(const z of diag.zones){
      let sx=0,sy=0,n=0;
      const x0=Math.floor((z.col-1)*GW/4),x1=Math.ceil(z.col*GW/4);
      const y0=Math.floor((z.row-1)*GH/4),y1=Math.ceil(z.row*GH/4);
      for(let y=y0;y<y1;y+=3)for(let x=x0;x<x1;x+=3){
        if(x>=0&&y>=0&&x<GW&&y<GH&&M.inP&&M.inP[idx(x,y)]){sx+=x;sy+=y;n++;}
      }
      if(n)features.push({
        type:"Feature",
        properties:{kind:"zone-label",label:z.id+" · "+z.accepted+" corridors"},
        geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:earthlineGridToLngLat(sx/n,sy/n)}
      });
    }
    return features;
  }
  function earthlineComputeCoverage15803(diag,swales,maxS,chanThr){
    const corridorMask=earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales);
    let corridorPixels=0;for(const v of corridorMask)corridorPixels+=v?1:0;
    const thresholds=[
      {key:"within25ft",ft:25,m:25/3.280839895},
      {key:"within50ft",ft:50,m:50/3.280839895},
      {key:"within100ft",ft:100,m:100/3.280839895}
    ];
    const counts={within25ft:0,within50ft:0,within100ft:0};
    const dist=corridorPixels?earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask):null;
    let safe=0;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;

    function buildCanonicalMinorPaths15836(){
      const eligible=new Uint8Array(N),upstream=new Uint16Array(N);
      for(let y=1;y<GH-1;y++)for(let x=1;x<GW-1;x++){
        const i=idx(x,y),a=Number(M.acc&&M.acc[i]||0),d=Number(M.dir&&M.dir[i]);
        if(M.inP[i]&&a>=thr&&a<chanThr&&d>=0&&d<8)eligible[i]=1;
      }
      for(let y=1;y<GH-1;y++)for(let x=1;x<GW-1;x++){
        const i=idx(x,y);if(!eligible[i])continue;
        const d=Number(M.dir[i]),nx=x+D8X[d],ny=y+D8Y[d];
        if(nx>=0&&ny>=0&&nx<GW&&ny<GH){const ni=idx(nx,ny);if(eligible[ni])upstream[ni]++;}
      }
      const heads=[];
      for(let i=0;i<N;i++)if(eligible[i]&&upstream[i]===0)heads.push(i);
      heads.sort((a,b)=>Number(M.acc[b]||0)-Number(M.acc[a]||0)||a-b);
      const claimed=new Uint8Array(N),paths=[];
      const trace=(start)=>{
        const cells=[],seen=new Set();let i=start,guard=0;
        while(i>=0&&i<N&&eligible[i]&&!seen.has(i)&&guard++<N){
          seen.add(i);cells.push(i);claimed[i]=1;
          const x=i%GW,y=Math.floor(i/GW),d=Number(M.dir[i]);
          if(!(d>=0&&d<8))break;
          const nx=x+D8X[d],ny=y+D8Y[d];
          if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=GW||ny>=GH)break;
          const ni=idx(nx,ny);if(!eligible[ni])break;i=ni;
        }
        return cells;
      };
      for(const h of heads){const cells=trace(h);if(cells.length>=2)paths.push(cells);}
      for(let i=0;i<N;i++)if(eligible[i]&&!claimed[i]){const cells=trace(i);if(cells.length>=2)paths.push(cells);}
      paths.sort((a,b)=>Number(M.acc[b[b.length-1]]||0)-Number(M.acc[a[a.length-1]]||0)||a[0]-b[0]);
      return paths.map((cells,k)=>{
        const pathMask16320=new Uint8Array(N);
        for(const c16320 of cells)if(c16320>=0&&c16320<N)pathMask16320[c16320]=1;
        const probeCells16320=cells.slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(M.acc[b]||0)-Number(M.acc[a]||0));
        return {id:"T"+(k+1),cells,pathMask16320,probeCells16320,peakAccumulation:Math.max(...cells.map(c=>Number(M.acc[c]||0)))};
      });
    }

    function segmentCrossesPath15836(seg,pathCells,tol=1,pathMask16320=null){
      if(!Array.isArray(seg)||seg.length<2||!Array.isArray(pathCells)||!pathCells.length)return false;
      const pathSet16288=pathMask16320?null:new Set(pathCells);
      for(let p=1;p<seg.length;p++){
        const a=seg[p-1],b=seg[p],steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0]),Math.abs(b[1]-a[1]))*2));
        for(let k=0;k<=steps;k++){
          const q=k/steps,x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*q,y=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*q;
          const minX=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(x-tol)),maxX=Math.min(GW-1,Math.floor(x+tol));
          const minY=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(y-tol)),maxY=Math.min(GH-1,Math.floor(y+tol));
          for(let yy=minY;yy<=maxY;yy++)for(let xx=minX;xx<=maxX;xx++){
            const pi16320=idx(xx,yy);
            if(pathMask16320?!!pathMask16320[pi16320]:pathSet16288.has(pi16320))return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

  function earthlineCoverageRecordCandidate15803(diag,pts,status,reason,levelIndex,extra={}){

      ((extra&&extra.placementMode15836)==="flow-first"||Number(levelIndex)===-1?"flow-first":"contour");

      id:"C"+diag.spatial.candidates.length,

      levelIndex:Number(levelIndex),status:normalizedStatus15840,

      candidateClass15840:selectionEligible15840?"selection-eligible":"generation-rejection",
      seedId15840:null,

      reachedFinalCapEvaluation15840:false

      if(origin15840==="flow-first")z.flowFirstCandidates++;

    }
    diag.spatial.candidates.push(rec);
    if(rec.seedId15840){

  }

  function earthlineFinalizeCandidateAccounting15840(diag){
    if(!diag||!diag.spatial||!Array.isArray(diag.spatial.candidates))return null;
    const records15840=diag.spatial.candidates;
    const selection15840=records15840.filter(r=>r&&r.candidateClass15840==="selection-eligible");
    const flowFirst15840=selection15840.filter(r=>r.candidateOrigin15840==="flow-first");
    const contour15840=selection15840.filter(r=>r.candidateOrigin15840!=="flow-first");
    const rejectedGeneration15840=records15840.filter(r=>r&&r.candidateClass15840==="generation-rejection");
    for(const zone15840 of diag.zones||[]){
      zone15840.flowFirstCandidates=selection15840.filter(r=>r.zone===zone15840.id&&r.candidateOrigin15840==="flow-first").length;
      zone15840.contourGeneratedCandidates=selection15840.filter(r=>r.zone===zone15840.id&&r.candidateOrigin15840!=="flow-first").length;
      zone15840.candidates=zone15840.flowFirstCandidates+zone15840.contourGeneratedCandidates;
    }
    const accounting15840={
      definition:"selection-eligible candidate records that entered the final spacing/acceptance queue; generation-stage rejection records are reported separately",
      candidateRecordsTotal:selection15840.length,
      flowFirstCandidates:flowFirst15840.length,
      contourGeneratedCandidates:contour15840.length,
      generationRejectionRecords:rejectedGeneration15840.length,
      spatialCandidateRecordsAllStages:records15840.length,
      candidateCountReconciles:selection15840.length===flowFirst15840.length+contour15840.length
    };
    diag.candidateAccounting15840=accounting15840;
    Object.assign(diag.totals,accounting15840);
    return accounting15840;
  }

  function earthlineFinalizeSeedFates15840(diag){
    if(!diag||!diag.spatial)return null;
    const seeds15840=Array.isArray(diag.spatial.seeds)?diag.spatial.seeds:[];
    const candidates15840=Array.isArray(diag.spatial.candidates)?diag.spatial.candidates:[];
    const bySeed15840=new Map();
    for(const candidate15840 of candidates15840){
      const seedId15840=candidate15840&&candidate15840.seedId15840;
      if(!seedId15840)continue;
      if(!bySeed15840.has(seedId15840))bySeed15840.set(seedId15840,[]);
      bySeed15840.get(seedId15840).push(candidate15840);
    }
    const priority15840=[
      "accepted","rejected-final-spacing","not-evaluated-final-cap",
      "rejected-conversion","rejected-overlap","rejected-short","candidate"
    ];
    for(const seed15840 of seeds15840){
      const linked15840=(bySeed15840.get(seed15840.id)||[]).slice();
      seed15840.candidateIds15840=linked15840.map(r=>r.id);
      seed15840.candidateOutcomes15840=linked15840.map(r=>({
        candidateId:r.id,status:r.status,reason:r.reason||null,
        origin:r.candidateOrigin15840||null,
        finalCorridorId:r.finalCorridorId15843||null,
        finalCorridorIndex:Number.isFinite(Number(r.finalCorridorIndex15843))?Number(r.finalCorridorIndex15843):null,
        reachedSpacingEvaluation:!!r.reachedSpacingEvaluation15840,
        reachedFinalCapEvaluation:!!r.reachedFinalCapEvaluation15840,
        accepted:!!r.accepted
      }));
      seed15840.reachedSpacingEvaluation15840=linked15840.some(r=>!!r.reachedSpacingEvaluation15840);
      seed15840.reachedFinalCapEvaluation15840=linked15840.some(r=>!!r.reachedFinalCapEvaluation15840);
      seed15840.accepted15840=linked15840.some(r=>!!r.accepted||r.status==="accepted");
      let stage15840="generated",reason15840="pending";
      if(!seed15840.traceAttempted15840){
        stage15840=seed15840.status==="not-visited-per-level-cap"?"not-visited-per-level-cap":"not-traced";
        reason15840=seed15840.status||"trace-not-attempted";
      }else if(seed15840.traceResult15840==="no-trace"){
        stage15840="no-trace";reason15840="trace-returned-fewer-than-three-points";
      }else if(!seed15840.segmentProduced15840||Number(seed15840.segmentCount15840||0)===0){
        stage15840="trace-produced-no-segment";reason15840="no-usable-segments";
      }else if(!linked15840.length){
        stage15840="segment-produced-no-candidate";
        reason15840=(seed15840.segmentTerminalCodes15840||[]).join("+")||"no-candidate-record";
      }else{
        const chosen15840=priority15840.map(status15840=>linked15840.find(r=>r.status===status15840)).find(Boolean)||linked15840[0];
        if(chosen15840.status==="accepted")stage15840="accepted";
        else if(chosen15840.status==="not-evaluated-final-cap")stage15840="rejected-final-cap";
        else stage15840=chosen15840.status||"candidate-not-finalized";
        reason15840=chosen15840.reason||stage15840;
      }
      seed15840.terminalStage15840=stage15840;
      seed15840.terminalReasonCode15840=reason15840;
      seed15840.fateFinalized15840=true;
    }
    diag.seedFateSummary15840={
      instrument:"WHY NOT HERE seed-fate pipeline",
      observationalOnly:true,
      seedCount:seeds15840.length,
      terminalStageCounts:seeds15840.reduce((acc15840,seed15840)=>{
        const key15840=seed15840.terminalStage15840||"unknown";
        acc15840[key15840]=(acc15840[key15840]||0)+1;
        return acc15840;
      },{})
    };
    return diag.seedFateSummary15840;
  }
  function earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx){
    let lo=0,hi=levels.length-1;
    while(lo<=hi){
      const mid=(lo+hi)>>1,v=levels[mid];
      if(v<mn)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid-1;
    }
    return lo<levels.length&&levels[lo]<=mx;
  }
  function earthlineMeasureContourCells15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr){
    if(!diag||!levels||!levels.length)return;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS)continue;
      const h00=Number(M.h[idx(x,y)]),h10=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y)]);
      const h01=Number(M.h[idx(x,y+1)]),h11=Number(M.h[idx(x+1,y+1)]);
      if(![h00,h10,h01,h11].every(Number.isFinite))continue;
      const mn=Math.min(h00,h10,h01,h11),mx=Math.max(h00,h10,h01,h11);
      if(mx-mn>1e-7&&earthlineCoverageHasLevel15803(levels,mn,mx)){
        earthlineCoverageZone15803(diag,x,y).contourCells++;
        diag.totals.contourCells++;
      }
    }
  }
  function earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales){
    const mask=new Uint8Array(Math.max(1,N));
    const mark=(x,y)=>{
      const ix=Math.round(x),iy=Math.round(y);
      if(ix>=0&&iy>=0&&ix<GW&&iy<GH)mask[idx(ix,iy)]=1;
    };
    for(const sw of (swales||[])){
      const pts=sw&&sw.pts||[];
      for(let j=0;j<pts.length;j++){
        mark(pts[j][0],pts[j][1]);
        if(j===0)continue;
        const a=pts[j-1],b=pts[j];
        const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0]),Math.abs(b[1]-a[1]))*2));
        for(let k=1;k<steps;k++){
          const t=k/steps;mark(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t);
        }
      }
    }
    return mask;
  }
  function earthlineEDT1D15803(f,n){
    const v=new Int32Array(n),z=new Float64Array(n+1),d=new Float64Array(n);
    let k=0;v[0]=0;z[0]=-Infinity;z[1]=Infinity;
    for(let q=1;q<n;q++){
      let s;
      do{
        const p=v[k];
        s=((f[q]+q*q)-(f[p]+p*p))/(2*q-2*p);
        if(s<=z[k])k--;
      }while(s<=z[k]&&k>=0);
      k++;v[k]=q;z[k]=s;z[k+1]=Infinity;
    }
    k=0;
    for(let q=0;q<n;q++){
      while(z[k+1]<q)k++;
      const p=v[k];d[q]=(q-p)*(q-p)+f[p];
    }
    return d;
  }
  function earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask){
    const BIG=1e12,tmp=new Float64Array(N),out=new Float64Array(N);
    for(let y=0;y<GH;y++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)f[x]=corridorMask[idx(x,y)]?0:BIG;
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GW);
      for(let x=0;x<GW;x++)tmp[idx(x,y)]=d[x];
    }
    for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
      const f=new Float64Array(GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)f[y]=tmp[idx(x,y)];
      const d=earthlineEDT1D15803(f,GH);
      for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)out[idx(x,y)]=Math.sqrt(d[y])*Math.max(.001,Number(M.cellM||1));
    }
    return out;
  }
  function earthlineBuildBoundaryEvidence15803(diag){
    const features=[];
    let poly=(M.analysisBoundaryLngLat||[]).map(p=>[Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])]).filter(p=>p.every(Number.isFinite));
    const frame15875=M.authoritativePropertyFrame15821||null;
    let acres15875=Number(frame15875&&frame15875.acres);
    if(!Number.isFinite(acres15875)||acres15875<=0)acres15875=Number(M.loc&&M.loc.acres);
    if((!Number.isFinite(acres15875)||acres15875<=0)&&poly.length>=3&&typeof earthlinePolygonAreaM2==="function"){
      acres15875=earthlinePolygonAreaM2(poly)/4046.8564224;
    }
    const roundedAcres15875=Number.isFinite(acres15875)&&acres15875>0
      ?(Math.abs(acres15875-Math.round(acres15875))<0.05?String(Math.round(acres15875)):acres15875.toFixed(1))
      :"";
    const boundaryLabel15875=roundedAcres15875?roundedAcres15875+"-acre boundary":"Analysis boundary";
    if(poly.length>=3){
      if(poly[0][0]!==poly[poly.length-1][0]||poly[0][1]!==poly[poly.length-1][1])poly.push(poly[0].slice());
      features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"boundary",label:"Exact analysis boundary",boundaryLabel:boundaryLabel15875},geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:poly}});
      diag.boundary={type:"analysisBoundaryLngLat",coordinates:poly,label:boundaryLabel15875};
    }else{
      const ring=[[0,0],[GW,0],[GW,GH],[0,GH],[0,0]].map(p=>earthlineGridToLngLat(p[0],p[1]));
      features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"boundary",label:"Analysis-grid extent (boundary unavailable)",boundaryLabel:boundaryLabel15875},geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:ring}});
      diag.boundary={type:"gridExtentFallback",coordinates:ring,label:boundaryLabel15875};
    }
    const addClipped=(vertical,pos,index)=>{
      let active=null;
      const limit=vertical?GH:GW;
      for(let a=0;a<=limit;a++){
        const x=vertical?Math.max(0,Math.min(GW-1,Math.round(pos))):Math.max(0,Math.min(GW-1,a));
        const y=vertical?Math.max(0,Math.min(GH-1,a)):Math.max(0,Math.min(GH-1,Math.round(pos)));
        const inside=a<limit&&M.inP&&M.inP[idx(x,y)];
        if(inside&&!active)active=[];
        if(inside)active.push(earthlineGridToLngLat(vertical?pos:a,vertical?a:pos));
        if((!inside||a===limit)&&active){
          if(active.length>=2)features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"grid",index},geometry:{type:"LineString",coordinates:active}});
          active=null;
        }
      }
    };
    for(let i=1;i<4;i++){addClipped(true,GW*i/4,"V"+i);addClipped(false,GH*i/4,"H"+i);}
    for(const z of diag.zones){
      let sx=0,sy=0,n=0;
      const x0=Math.floor((z.col-1)*GW/4),x1=Math.ceil(z.col*GW/4);
      const y0=Math.floor((z.row-1)*GH/4),y1=Math.ceil(z.row*GH/4);
      for(let y=y0;y<y1;y+=3)for(let x=x0;x<x1;x+=3){
        if(x>=0&&y>=0&&x<GW&&y<GH&&M.inP&&M.inP[idx(x,y)]){sx+=x;sy+=y;n++;}
      }
      if(n)features.push({
        type:"Feature",
        properties:{kind:"zone-label",label:z.id+" · "+z.accepted+" corridors"},
        geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:earthlineGridToLngLat(sx/n,sy/n)}
      });
    }
    return features;
  }
  function earthlineComputeCoverage15803(diag,swales,maxS,chanThr){
    const corridorMask=earthlineRasterizeCorridors15803(swales);
    let corridorPixels=0;for(const v of corridorMask)corridorPixels+=v?1:0;
    const thresholds=[
      {key:"within25ft",ft:25,m:25/3.280839895},
      {key:"within50ft",ft:50,m:50/3.280839895},
      {key:"within100ft",ft:100,m:100/3.280839895}
    ];
    const counts={within25ft:0,within50ft:0,within100ft:0};
    const dist=corridorPixels?earthlineDistanceField15803(corridorMask):null;
    let safe=0;
    for(let y=2;y<GH-2;y++)for(let x=2;x<GW-2;x++){
      const i=idx(x,y);
      if(!M.inP[i]||M.pond[i]||(M.noBuildMask&&M.noBuildMask[i]))continue;
      if((M.acc[i]||0)>=chanThr||slopePct(x,y)>maxS||!Number.isFinite(Number(M.h[i])))continue;
      safe++;
      const z=earthlineCoverageZone15803(diag,x,y);
      const dm=dist?dist[i]:Infinity;
      for(const t of thresholds)if(dm<=t.m){counts[t.key]++;z.coverage[t.key]++;}
    }
    const pct={};
    for(const t of thresholds)pct[t.key]=safe?100*counts[t.key]/safe:0;
    for(const z of diag.zones){
      for(const t of thresholds)z.coverage[t.key+"Pct"]=z.safeCells?100*z.coverage[t.key]/z.safeCells:0;
    }
    diag.coverage={
      method:"exact Euclidean grid distance to rasterized accepted corridor centerlines",
      cellM:Number(M.cellM||0),safeCells:safe,corridorPixels,
      counts,pct,
      within25ftPct:pct.within25ft,
      within50ftPct:pct.within50ft,
      within100ftPct:pct.within100ft
    };
  }
  function earthlineFinalizeCoverageDiagnostic15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr,kept,checked){
    if(!diag)return null;
    diag.totals.accepted=kept.length;
    diag.termination.levelsTotal=levels.length;
    diag.termination.levelsProcessed=checked;
    diag.termination.levelsUnprocessed=Math.max(0,levels.length-checked);
    diag.termination.timeBudgetReached=!!(M.swaleDiagnostics&&M.swaleDiagnostics.timeBudgetReached);
    diag.termination.truncated=!!(
      diag.termination.candidateCapReached||
      diag.termination.finalCapReached||
      diag.termination.timeBudgetReached
    );
    const reasons=[];
    if(diag.termination.candidateCapReached)reasons.push("candidate-cap");
    if(diag.termination.finalCapReached)reasons.push("final-cap");
    if(diag.termination.timeBudgetReached)reasons.push("time-budget");
    if(!reasons.length)reasons.push(diag.termination.candidateStage||"exhausted");
    diag.termination.reason=reasons.join("+");
    earthlineFinalizeCandidateAccounting15840(diag);
    earthlineFinalizeSeedFates15840(diag);
    earthlineFinalizePreselectionFate15841(diag);
    earthlineMeasureContourCells15803(diag,levels,maxS,chanThr);
    earthlineComputeCoverage15803(diag,kept,maxS,chanThr);
    diag.mapFeatures=earthlineBuildBoundaryEvidence15803(diag);
    diag.runtimeBuild=String(window.EARTHLINE_BUILD||diag.runtimeBuild||"");
    diag.noBuildProvenance15843=diag.noBuildProvenance15843||null;
    diag.completedAt=new Date().toISOString();
    M.coverageDiagnostic15803=diag;
    return diag;
  }

  }
  function earthlineRenderCoverageDiagnostic15803(){
    const diag=M.coverageDiagnostic15803;
    if(!diag||!earthlineEnsureCoverageLayers15803())return false;
    try{if(earthlineMap.getLayer(COVERAGE_TARGET_15803))earthlineMap.setLayoutProperty(COVERAGE_TARGET_15803,"visibility","none");}catch(_){}
    const features=(diag.mapFeatures||[]).filter(f=>String(f&&f.properties&&f.properties.kind||"")!=="grid"&&String(f&&f.properties&&f.properties.kind||"")!=="zone-label");
    const target=diag.whyNotHere;
    if(target&&Number.isFinite(target.lng)&&Number.isFinite(target.lat)){
      features.push({type:"Feature",properties:{kind:"target",status:target.status||""},geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:[target.lng,target.lat]}});

        features.push({
          type:"Feature",

          geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:earthlineGridToLngLat(rec.x,rec.y)}
        });
      }
